State-shaped signals act **exactly once per number** (`already_opted_out`), which is what stops `has_opted_out=true` from re-attributing a months-old opt-out on every tick.
- **Two Phase-1 bugs found by live recon and fixed:** the healthcheck parsed a guessed `data`/`content` wrapper, but every TR collection is `{items,meta}` — it returned zero dashboards off a healthy 200; and the docs pointed at a `docs/superpowers/textrequest-integration-brief.md` that does not exist (link removed).
- API contract established by reading the live API against its OpenAPI spec (`/dist/swagger/apiv3docs.json`; the docs page is client-rendered), recorded in `scripts/probe-textrequest-api*.ts`. Key hazard documented in 06-integrations: **TR silently IGNORES unknown query params**, so a guessed date-filter name yields the account's whole history instead of a window; and every timestamp is UTC with **no `Z`**, so it must be parsed via `parseTxrUtcTimestamp` (the TextHub Mountain-time bug class).
- `supports_api_send` stays **false**; migrations 0121–0124 are NOT applied yet (they are additive, and `0122`/`0124` take FK locks on `contacts`/`stage_sends`, so apply when the drain is idle). Verified: `tsc` clean; 131 checks green across `scripts/test-textrequest-{send,dlr,poll,optout}.ts` (the DB-backed halves apply the pending migrations inside a rolled-back transaction via `scripts/_txr-migration-fixture.ts`, which sets a short `lock_timeout` after a first version deadlocked against live production traffic); `verify-migration-integrity` clean except the expected "not yet applied" rows for 0121–0124. (ClickUp 869e97atu.)

## 2026-07-25 — Text Request (`txr`) provider — Phase 1 skeleton (migration 0120) — docs: 06-integrations, 07-conventions, 03-data-model, CHANGELOG
- New SMS provider **Text Request** registered as `txr` (`lib/sends/providers/textrequest.ts`, `ADAPTERS['txr']`). Phase 1 is a skeleton only: `send()` is a clean not-implemented stub (never throws), `parseDlr`/`parseInbound` return `null`, and the sole live capability is a non-sending **healthcheck** (`GET /dashboards`, `x-api-key` header) behind the account "Check connection" button (`.../credentials/[credentialId]/healthcheck`, txr-gated). `supports_api_send` stays **false** until Phase 2 go-live (real `POST /messages` + per-message `status_callback`). Adapter contract extended additively: `NormalizedSendParams.statusCallbackUrl?` (drain-built for TR's per-message callback; other adapters ignore it) + key union widened to include `txr`. `Send test` is now gated to TextHub keys (`txh`/`txh2`) — also hides the TextHub-hardcoded button for Ahoi. Migration 0120: seeds the `txr` `sms_providers` row (idempotent, `supports_api_send=false`, `1500`/min, `30000`/24h) + adds nullable `provider_phones.dashboard_id` text (TR is dashboard-scoped, one dashboard per number; resolved stage→phone→dashboard at send time in Phase 2). Number + credential + the 25 MPS TFN rate (`provider_phones.max_sends_per_second`) are set via the UI, not the migration. Verified: `tsc` clean, `getAdapter` regression (txr/txh/txh2/ahi + unknown→throw), migration-integrity OK. (ClickUp 869e97atu.)

## 2026-07-25 — Per-campaign opt-out-rate auto-pause breaker (migration 0119, P7/P8) — docs: 04-features/sms-send-pipeline, 03-data-model (+ERD), 06-integrations, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- New third-scope latching send breaker on the `campaigns` row (`send_paused`/`send_paused_reason`/`send_paused_at`) with its own append-only `campaign_circuit_events` audit table (mirror of `send_circuit_events` at campaign scope). Every opt-out ingestion path (TextHub [poll-opt-outs.ts](../lib/sends/poll-opt-outs.ts), Ahoi [ahoi-optout.ts](../lib/sends/ahoi-optout.ts) / [ahoi-cdr-poll.ts](../lib/sends/ahoi-cdr-poll.ts)) that attributes a STOP to a stage recomputes the campaign's trailing-window opt-out rate (`opt_out_attributions ÷ stage_sends sent`, same `OPTOUT_RATE_WINDOW_SEC` window both sides) and latches `campaigns.send_paused` when `sent ≥ OPTOUT_RATE_MIN_SENDS` (200) **and** `rate ≥ OPTOUT_RATE_SPIKE_THRESHOLD` (0.10) — [optout-rate-breaker.ts](../lib/sends/optout-rate-breaker.ts) + [circuit-breakers.ts](../lib/sends/circuit-breakers.ts). Latch in-tx, Telegram alert (rate + counts + campaign link) post-commit. ANDed with the provider latch across [scheduled.ts](../lib/sends/scheduled.ts) (Phase A + drain selection), [drain.ts](../lib/sends/drain.ts) (pre-claim `campaign_paused` refusal + mid-run kill), and [stall-detector.ts](../lib/sends/stall-detector.ts) (paused ≠ stalled) — a campaign pause holds only that campaign; the provider latch is untouched. Manual-only resume: `POST /api/campaigns/[campaignId]/send-circuit` (perm `campaigns.pause`), audited. Also fixed the previously-silent Ahoi DLR reject-spike latch to fire the same Telegram alert. Migration 0119 (additive: 3 nullable/defaulted campaigns columns + the new table). Verified by scripts/test-optout-rate-breaker.ts (15). (ClickUp 869e989gf, P7/P8.)

## 2026-07-24 — Preflight-to-Telegram + Autopilot weekly view (migration 0118, P5/P6) — docs: 04-features/sms-send-pipeline, 04-features/crons, 03-data-model, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- New send-preflight cron ([lib/sends/send-preflight.ts](../lib/sends/send-preflight.ts), `/api/cron/send-preflight` `*/5`) computes each scheduled stage's resolved-audience + exclusion breakdown (`computePreflightBreakdown`, [preflight-breakdown.ts](../lib/sends/preflight-breakdown.ts)) ~15 min before it materializes, persists it, and posts a Telegram digest + standalone red alerts. The breakdown reuses `preflightStageSend` (audience + blockers + drain estimate) and `computeStageReconciliation` (opt-out/content-dedup/split buckets) and adds two NET-NEW numbers: the **lane** exclusion count and a **predictive 1-hour phone-dedup** count (`dedup_1h_predicted`) — surfacing a ~100%-dedup child BEFORE it fires. Autopilot view ([/api/sends/autopilot](../app/api/sends/autopilot/route.ts) + [/sends/autopilot page](../app/(protected)/sends/autopilot/page.tsx)): 7-day read-model with slip state, the parent-complete gate (`getParentState`), and the persisted preflight; new **`held`** operational status ([stage-status.ts](../lib/stages/stage-status.ts)); **release-hold** + **preflight-abort** endpoints (net-new — no endpoint touched `slip_hold_at` before). `preflight_aborted_at` added to Phase A's due-selection gate. Migration 0118 adds `preflight_result`/`preflight_computed_at`/`preflight_notified_at`/`preflight_aborted_at` to `campaign_stages` (additive/nullable). Verified by scripts/test-preflight-breakdown.ts (12) + scripts/test-send-preflight.ts (5). (ClickUp 869e8rxt7.)

## 2026-07-24 — Drain resilience: phone-partitioned concurrency, round-robin, stall alert, build-time guardrail — docs: 04-features/sms-send-pipeline, 04-features/crons, CHANGELOG
- Long-term follow-up to the head-of-line fast fix. **(1) Phone-partitioned concurrent drain (Phase 1):** Phase B groups drainable stages by `provider_phone_id` and drains groups concurrently (`mapWithConcurrency`, ≤8), so a slow number never blocks a fast one; per-provider budget is a shared atomic reservation (`makeProviderBudget`). **(2) Round-robin within a phone (Phase 2):** same-phone stages take turns in 20s slices so none starves a sibling. **(3) Backlog-stall alert (Phase 3):** `lib/sends/stall-detector.ts` `findStalledStages`, wired into the hourly `telegram-report` cron — Telegrams when a stage that should be draining has sent nothing for 30 min (in-window, not paused), the catch-all for any silent drain stall. **(4) Build-time guardrail (Phase 4):** preflight returns `sender_sends_per_second` / `estimated_drain_seconds` / `warnings` and the Prepare dialog shows an amber warning when a big audience is on a low-rate number. No schema change. Files: [lib/sends/scheduled.ts](../lib/sends/scheduled.ts), [lib/sends/stall-detector.ts](../lib/sends/stall-detector.ts), [lib/sends/preflight.ts](../lib/sends/preflight.ts), [app/api/cron/telegram-report/route.ts](../app/api/cron/telegram-report/route.ts), [components/campaigns/stage-prepare-dialog.tsx](../components/campaigns/stage-prepare-dialog.tsx). Verified by scripts/test-scheduled-phone-concurrency.ts, scripts/test-stall-detector.ts, scripts/test-preflight-estimate.ts, scripts/verify-drain.ts.

## 2026-07-24 — Phase B fairness time-box (head-of-line-blocking fix) — docs: 04-features/sms-send-pipeline, CHANGELOG
- The `send-scheduled` cron's Phase B drains stages **sequentially**; one stage on a **slow phone number** (3/s toll-free) at the head of the queue was consuming the whole ~300s invocation and starving ~60K messages on 60/s short codes behind it for 41 minutes (live 2026-07-24). Fix: [lib/sends/drain.ts](../lib/sends/drain.ts) `runStageDrain` gains a `maxDurationMs` fairness slice (checked between batches, at-most-once preserved); [lib/sends/scheduled.ts](../lib/sends/scheduled.ts) Phase B caps each stage at `PER_STAGE_DRAIN_MS` (20s) and the whole phase at `PHASE_B_DEADLINE_MS` (240s). Yielded stages keep their `pending` rows (soft stop) and resume next tick; each stage still paces by its own phone's `max_sends_per_second`. No schema change. Verified by a new time-box case in [scripts/verify-drain.ts](../scripts/verify-drain.ts).
- The `skipped_opted_out` (STOP-cancel) and `skipped_duplicate` (1h dedup) suppression buckets — previously returned by the APIs but not shown — now render where sent/failed already are: the **Today** dashboard row ([app/(protected)/sends/today/page.tsx](../app/(protected)/sends/today/page.tsx): `· N STOP-cancel` / `· N skipped (1h)`, shown only when > 0) and the **stage send panel** ([components/campaigns/stage-send-panel.tsx](../components/campaigns/stage-send-panel.tsx): a compact suppression line under the count tiles). The stage-send API ([app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/[stageId]/send/route.ts](../app/api/campaigns/%5BcampaignId%5D/stages/%5BstageId%5D/send/route.ts)) now also returns `skipped_duplicate` alongside `skipped_opted_out`. No schema change. Follow-up to the P2 data layer (migration 0116).

## 2026-07-23 — Parent-complete gate + bounded slip for lane children (migration 0117) — docs: 04-features/sms-send-pipeline, 03-data-model, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- Behavioral lane children (`parent_stage_id` set) are now gated in the scheduler: Phase A only materializes a child once its parent is COMPLETE (`sent_at` set AND no `pending`/`sending` rows — `failed`/`skipped_*` don't block). If the parent overruns, the child is **slipped** to `parent_complete + original_offset`, quiet-hours-aware via new `nextWindowOpenAtOrAfter` ([lib/quiet-hours.ts](../lib/quiet-hours.ts)) which rolls to the next ET day, capped at **24h** past the original then **HELD** (`slip_hold_at`, parked for a human — never fired truncated, never burned as missed). A provider paused mid-drain freezes the parent's `pending` rows → child holds at 24h with a self-sufficient Telegram alert (the intended fail-safe; P8 blast-radius policy call). Pure decision in [lib/sends/child-slip.ts](../lib/sends/child-slip.ts) `decideChildSlip` (two-regime: fire at original when the parent was ready in time, slip to now+offset only when the child actively waited); wired into [lib/sends/scheduled.ts](../lib/sends/scheduled.ts) with `getParentState` + slip/hold Telegram + `slip_slipped`/`slip_waiting`/`slip_held` run counters. Migration 0117 adds `slip_original_scheduled_at`/`slip_count`/`slip_hold_at`/`slip_hold_reason` to `campaign_stages` (all additive/nullable). Verified by [scripts/test-child-slip.ts](../scripts/test-child-slip.ts) (16 checks: decisions, cross-day placement, 24h holds, mixed sent+failed parent = complete, child excludes failed contact, end-to-end stranded-parent hold). (P4 of the week-ahead pre-build work; ClickUp 869e8rwx4.)

## 2026-07-23 — Send-time opt-out invariant + `skipped_opted_out` status (migration 0116) — docs: 04-features/sms-send-pipeline, 03-data-model, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- The drain now re-checks `opt_outs` for each claimed batch immediately before the 1-hour dedup gate ([lib/sends/drain.ts](../lib/sends/drain.ts)), marking any contact who opted out AFTER materialization as the new terminal status **`skipped_opted_out`** (`last_error='opt_out_cancel'`) — never sent. Closes the materialize→dispatch window that let a pre-built/scheduled stage text a number that STOPped after the stage was materialized. The opt-out ingesters ([poll-opt-outs.ts](../lib/sends/poll-opt-outs.ts), [ahoi-optout.ts](../lib/sends/ahoi-optout.ts)) also cascade-cancel still-`pending` rows for the contact on intake. Applies identically to scheduled + manual sends (shared `runStageDrain`). Distinct bucket surfaced in the stage-panel + Today endpoints + scheduled-run result; migration 0116 extends the `stage_sends_status_check` CHECK. Verified by [scripts/test-send-optout-invariant.ts](../scripts/test-send-optout-invariant.ts). (P2 of the week-ahead pre-build work; ClickUp 869e8rwx4.)

- `/api/campaigns/list` free-text search ([app/api/campaigns/list/route.ts](../app/api/campaigns/list/route.ts)) added `tracking_id` to the `ILIKE` `or()` clause (was `name`/`human_id`/`slug` only), so campaigns are findable by their tracking ID (e.g. `8_58_072026_1`). Search-box placeholder updated on `app/(protected)/campaigns/page.tsx`.

- The per-account "Send a test SMS" dialog ([components/providers/provider-credentials-section.tsx](../components/providers/provider-credentials-section.tsx)) gained a **"Send from"** dropdown listing the account's active linked numbers (plus "Account default"). The route ([app/api/providers/[providerId]/credentials/test/route.ts](../app/api/providers/%5BproviderId%5D/credentials/test/route.ts)) resolves the chosen phone (verified active + linked to the account) to TextHub's `sender` via `toTexthubSender()`; omitting it keeps the prior account-default behavior. The result echoes which number it sent `from`. Optional field on `providerCredentialTestSchema`; no schema change.

## 2026-07-22 — Move a provider phone to another provider (reassign in place) — docs: 04-features/registry
- The Edit-phone dialog gained a **Provider** picker; changing it moves the number to another provider by reassigning `provider_phones.provider_id` on the same row (the `(org_id, phone_number)` unique constraint is never re-triggered, so shifting a number between vendors no longer hits "already exists"). `PATCH /api/providers/[providerId]/phones/[phoneId]` now accepts `provider_id` (target) + `confirm_move`; the move clears `credential_id` and re-attributes the number's past sends to the new provider in number-level reports. Not-yet-sent stages (`draft`/`pending`) trigger a `409` `details.reason = 'move_needs_confirmation'` → UI confirm → re-submit with `confirm_move: true`. **No schema/migration change** (column + all `provider_phones.id` FKs are `ON DELETE SET NULL` already). ([lib/validators/provider-phones.ts](../lib/validators/provider-phones.ts), [app/api/providers/[providerId]/phones/[phoneId]/route.ts](../app/api/providers/%5BproviderId%5D/phones/%5BphoneId%5D/route.ts), [components/providers/phone-form.tsx](../components/providers/phone-form.tsx))

Still stores only `default_provider_phone_id`; the provider is UI-only, derived from the stored phone on load. `GET /api/provider-phones/list` now also returns `provider_color` for the dot. No schema/API-contract change. ([components/campaigns/campaign-editor-page.tsx](../components/campaigns/campaign-editor-page.tsx))

## 2026-07-22 — TextHub sender selection (sender param from the stage's phone; campaign default sender prefill; API-send stages now require a sending number) — docs: 03-data-model, 04-features/sms-send-pipeline, 04-features/campaigns-stages-creatives, 06-integrations, 07-conventions
- **Sender param.** TextHub's `sender` = the send-from number as national digits, no country code, derived from the stage's `provider_phone_id` via `toTexthubSender()` ([lib/sends/texthub.ts](../lib/sends/texthub.ts)); the adapter ([lib/sends/providers/texthub.ts](../lib/sends/providers/texthub.ts)) refuses cleanly on a null sender, never falling back to TextHub's account default.
- **Kickoff + preflight gate.** Generalized the existing Ahoi-only `no_sender_number` kickoff guard ([lib/sends/kickoff.ts](../lib/sends/kickoff.ts)) to every API-send (tracked) stage — TextHub included — and added the matching "Sending number assigned" blocker to the read-only preflight checklist ([lib/sends/preflight.ts](../lib/sends/preflight.ts)). Pre-deploy audit: [scripts/audit-stages-missing-sender.ts](../scripts/audit-stages-missing-sender.ts).
- **Campaign default sender.** New `campaigns.default_provider_phone_id` (migration 0115, nullable FK → `provider_phones`, `ON DELETE SET NULL`) — prefill-only; a new stage under a campaign with a default set pre-fills its provider+phone (operator can override). Send-time resolution stays stage-only. New `GET /api/provider-phones/list` feeds the campaign form's picker.
- Verified: [scripts/test-texthub-send.ts](../scripts/test-texthub-send.ts) + tsc clean.

## 2026-07-22 — Campaign segments: per-segment include / exclude (migration 0114) — docs: 03-data-model, 04-features/audience-snapshot
- **Feature.** Each selected segment on a campaign can now **include** (narrow the audience to it) or **exclude** (subtract it), toggled per-segment on the segment chip. Audience math: `final = (include ∩ group) EXCEPT (union of exclude segments)`. A segment is include XOR exclude (disjoint). Group is the base for exclude; an exclude-only selection has no positive base → empty.
- **Data.** New `campaigns.audience_exclude_segment_ids int[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'` ([db/migrations/0114_campaign_exclude_segments.sql](../db/migrations/0114_campaign_exclude_segments.sql)). `audience_segment_ids` keeps its meaning = the include set. Additive + backward-compatible — existing campaigns default to no excludes, behavior unchanged.
- **Logic.** [lib/audience-snapshot.ts](../lib/audience-snapshot.ts): `buildAudienceSourceClause` gains an `excludeUnion` arg (`(P) EXCEPT (exclude)`); `buildExcludeSegmentUnion` evaluates exclude segments against the group universe (final ⊆ group — preserves the `restrictUniverse` perf lever); `previewAudience` adds a `from_exclude_segment` marker + `membership_ok = positive AND NOT from_exclude_segment` and reports `excluded_by_segments`. Threaded through create / PATCH / status / audience-preview / stages / stages-audience-preview / duplicate routes; `excludeSegmentIds` added to `AudiencePreviewInput`, `computeStageAudienceCountForDraft`, `computeStageAudienceCountsBatchForDraft`, `computeStageEligibilityPreview` draft, `snapshotAudience`.
- **Validation.** `audience_exclude_segment_ids` on create/update/preview schemas ([lib/validators/campaigns.ts](../lib/validators/campaigns.ts)), disjoint-from-include check, FK-ownership verified against org. Added to the PATCH audience-lock guard (`audience_locked_after_draft`).
- **UI.** `SegmentPicker` gains an optional per-chip Include/Exclude toggle (`segmentModes` + `onToggleMode`); exclude chips are red-tinted. Right-rail preview shows "Excluded by segment: N". Editor form carries the two arrays (union feeds the picker).
- **Verified.** [scripts/test-segment-exclude.ts](../scripts/test-segment-exclude.ts) against real data: partition `include(G∩S) + exclude(G\\S) == group(G)` (16862 + 86954 == 103816), `excluded_by_segments == include`, exclude-only → empty. Plus tsc + eslint (changed files) + `next build`.

## 2026-07-22 — Campaign create form UI upgrades (offer pin/recent picker, auto API Send, Preland default, Clickers pre-selected) — docs: 04-features/ui-system, 04-features/campaigns-stages-creatives
- **Offer picker.** Replaced the plain `Select` with a new single-select `OfferPicker` ([components/offers/offer-picker.tsx](../components/offers/offer-picker.tsx)) modeled on `SegmentPicker`: searchable, per-row pin star, Pinned → Recent → All ordering, `All/Recent/Pinned` tabs. Extracted the segment picker's localStorage logic into a generic `usePickerPrefs(namespace)` ([lib/hooks/use-picker-prefs.ts](../lib/hooks/use-picker-prefs.ts)); `useSegmentPrefs` is now a thin wrapper (no behavior change), `OfferPicker` uses the `offers` namespace.
- **Send method.** Create mode now **auto-selects `tracked` (API Send)** once a brand with an active short domain is chosen, respecting a manual override thereafter (new touched-ref + effect in [campaign-form-state.ts](../components/campaigns/campaign-form-state.ts)). Existing force-to-`manual` for domain-less brands unchanged.
- **Routing.** Create mode defaults `routing_type_id` to the `Preland` routing type when it exists (case-insensitive), else None; guarded on null so it composes with the single-option auto-select.
- **Audience filters.** `DEFAULT_FILTERS.include_clickers` flipped `false → true` (Clickers pill pre-selected on new campaigns). Edit mode loads saved filters, unaffected.
- All four are create-mode only. Spec: [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-22-campaign-form-ui-upgrades-design.md](superpowers/specs/2026-07-22-campaign-form-ui-upgrades-design.md).

## 2026-07-22 — Closed Supabase security-advisor ERRORs: RLS on 5 internal tables + SECURITY INVOKER on a report view (migration 0113) — docs: security-notes, 07-conventions
- **Trigger.** A Supabase advisor email flagged `rls_disabled_in_public` on `camman`. The full `get_advisors type=security` run showed **6 ERRORs**: five tables shipped with RLS off (`cron_locks`, `report_stage_hour`, `report_group_hour`, `report_refresh_log`, `carrier_norm_backfill_snapshot`) reachable read/write via the public Data API with the anon key, plus the `SECURITY DEFINER` view `offer_report_campaign_econ`.
- **Fix.** [db/migrations/0113_enable_rls_system_tables.sql](../db/migrations/0113_enable_rls_system_tables.sql): `ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` (no policy — deny-by-default, org-less infra) on the five tables; `ALTER VIEW … SET (security_invoker = true)` on the view. Safe because the app never uses PostgREST (zero `supabase.from()` data calls; supabase-js is Auth-only) — all five tables are server-only raw SQL over the direct Drizzle connection, which bypasses RLS. Same deny-by-default pattern as `geoip_cache`/`provider_credentials`.
- **Verified.** Applied to prod via `npm run db:migrate`; `verify-migration-integrity` all-green (113/113); re-ran the advisor → **0 ERRORs** (the 5 tables now report `rls_enabled_no_policy` INFO, the intended end-state). Remaining advisor WARNs (2 matviews in API, 7 SECURITY DEFINER RPC-callable functions, 1 mutable search_path, Auth leaked-password protection) logged as open hardening in security-notes.md — not addressed here.

## 2026-07-21 — TextHub inbox poll: paginate + per-credential fairness (fixes txh inbound-STOP starvation) — docs: 04-features/crons
- **Incident.** Inbound STOPs from the `txh` account stopped ingesting. Root cause: TextHub switched the inbox from a flat ~200-most-recent list to a **paginated, retained ~1,500-message window** (`&page=N`, `total_pages`, newest-first). The poller ([lib/sends/poll-opt-outs.ts](../lib/sends/poll-opt-outs.ts)) read only page 1 and processed credentials sequentially under one time cap, so when `txh2`'s volume spiked it consumed the budget and **starved `txh`** — txh's page 1 never drained and ~1,300 STOPs stranded on pages 2–8 that page-1-only reads can't reach (live compliance gap; txh ingested ~0 for hours).
- **Fix.** `fetchInbox({ page })` + `buildInboxUrl(page)` + `FetchInboxResult.page/totalPages` ([lib/sends/texthub-inbox.ts](../lib/sends/texthub-inbox.ts)); `pollCredential` walks pages **until caught up** (a page with 0 newly-claimed messages ⇒ everything older is already ingested, claiming being monotonic + newest-first), bounded per tick by `MAX_INBOX_PAGES` and a per-credential time budget `PER_CREDENTIAL_BUDGET_MS` (the fairness valve). Pure `shouldFetchNextInboxPage` termination logic (unit-tested). `maxDuration` 60→120. Also fixes the empty-inbox false alert (HTTP 200 `{"response":"No new messages"}` now = successful empty poll).
- **Recovery.** New one-shot [scripts/drain-texthub-inbox.ts](../scripts/drain-texthub-inbox.ts) paginates the whole window and ingests a deep backlog with the exact poller semantics (message-id claim, `source='sms_inbound'`, 72h attribution; dry-run default, `--apply`, idempotent). First run recovered **1,623** stranded STOPs (txh 1,458 / txh2 165) across 19 stages. Verified: [scripts/verify-poll-opt-outs.ts](../scripts/verify-poll-opt-outs.ts) (27 checks: pagination control + fetchInbox meta + empty-inbox classification) + tsc + eslint. Shipped to `main` (commits d0209ba, e4a34f1).

## 2026-07-21 — Moved the daily Telegram performance report from Warsaw 11:00 → Warsaw 10:00 (user preference; report was always DST-stable at 11:00 Warsaw, they want it an hour earlier). One-line change in [lib/reporting/telegram-report-format.ts](../lib/reporting/telegram-report-format.ts) `decideFormat` (`warsawHour === 11` → `=== 10`); hourly windows (16–23, 0–1) unchanged and non-overlapping with 10:00. Updated route doc comment + [scripts/test-telegram-report-decision.ts](../scripts/test-telegram-report-decision.ts) (all pass). Updated 04-features/crons.md.

## 2026-07-21 — Content-dedup LAYER 3 ("Exclude leads who already got this offer") is now baked into the FROZEN audience at activation, not just the preview. Root cause: `previewAudience` subtracted prior-offer leads (right-rail "N excluded") but `snapshotAudience`→`buildQualifierFromRelation` did NOT, so the frozen `campaign_audience_pool` kept them and the send-time anti-join re-removed them at materialization — the operator saw a large "3687 → 724" surprise drop, and the audience cap sampled the un-narrowed pool. Fix: `buildQualifierFromRelation` now applies the same offer-exposure exclusion as the preview (shared `flagSetCtes`/`flagJoins` `oe_set`, identical `is_eligible` predicate); both snapshot callers ([app/api/campaigns/route.ts](../app/api/campaigns/route.ts) create + [app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/status/route.ts](../app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/status/route.ts) draft→active) pass `excludePriorOffer` + `offerId`. Frozen pool now == previewed will-send; cap samples the narrowed pool. Send-time LAYER 3 kept as a live safety net (post-activation exposures + pools frozen before this change). Toggle OFF path is byte-identical (no `oe_set`). Verified: [scripts/test-snapshot-prior-offer.ts](../scripts/test-snapshot-prior-offer.ts) (snapshot ON==preview ON=87509, snapshot OFF==preview OFF=152055, delta==got_offer_in_prior_campaign=64546) + tsc. Updated 04-features/content-dedup.md.

## 2026-07-21 — "Add Opt-Outs" can now map imported numbers to the campaign/stage they replied STOP to. When the uploaded CSV includes a reply-time column (`received`/`received_at`/`time`), the opt-outs upload takes a new attribution path ([lib/sends/import-optout-attribution.ts](../lib/sends/import-optout-attribution.ts) `importOptOutsWithAttribution`): each opt-out is stored at its reply time and reverse-matched via the live poller's own `latestSendForAttribution` (single most-recent `sent` send within 72h; one STOP ⇒ one stage) → inserts `opt_out_attributions` + bumps stage `inbound_opt_out_count`/`opt_out_count` + recomputes cost. No in-window send ⇒ suppression only (`unattributed`). Dedup rules: (1) a number already in `opt_outs` is skipped entirely (no re-attribution to another campaign); (2) duplicate rows collapse to the earliest reply time. Opt-outs stay brand-scoped; naive timestamps interpreted in a chosen zone (ET default / Mountain for TextHub / UTC), ISO offsets honored. UI: `PhoneUploadForm` `captureReplyTime` prop (CSV 2nd column + timezone select) on the Add Opt-Outs dialog; the plain phone-list/Paste path is unchanged. Validator `optOutUploadSchema` gains optional `entries[]` + `timezone` (exactly one of phones/entries). No migration (reuses `opt_outs`/`opt_out_attributions`). Verified: scripts/test-optout-import-attribution.ts (dedup/skip/attribution/counters/idempotency, 21 checks) + tsc + eslint. Updated 04-features/csv-imports.md.

## 2026-07-20 — Stage tracking ID now previews live in EDIT mode too (not just create/split). Follow-up to the A/B-split fix below: a split variant created from a draft with no creative has a null stored `tracking_id`, and the edit form's `effectiveTrackingId` only ever returned the stored value — so picking a creative left the Tracking ID field empty and the `tracking_id` chip disabled until a save + reopen. Fixed in [components/campaigns/stage-form.tsx](../components/campaigns/stage-form.tsx): `effectiveTrackingId` now computes the live preview in edit mode using the stage's own `stage_number` (new `stageNumber` prop, passed from [stage-inline-creator.tsx](../components/campaigns/stage-inline-creator.tsx)) when no stored id exists — identical to create mode, and byte-identical to the server's on-save generation (`formatStageTrackingId` === `generateStageTrackingId`). A stored (immutable) id still shows verbatim. Placeholder/helper text updated accordingly. Client-only; no schema change. Verified: DB-confirmed the failing stage (campaign has tracking_id, variant had null creative/tracking) + tsc clean — updated 04-features/campaigns-stages-creatives, CHANGELOG.

## 2026-07-20 — A/B split now attaches variant A's tracking ID to its Full URL. The split endpoint ([app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/[stageId]/split/route.ts](../app/api/campaigns/%5BcampaignId%5D/stages/%5BstageId%5D/split/route.ts)) previously rebuilt only the new siblings B..N with `sub_id3=<own tracking id>`, leaving the source (variant A) as the bare sales-page URL — so operators had to reopen variant A after the split to attach the tag (easy to forget). Fixed by refactoring the sibling rebuild into one shared `attachStageTracking` helper and applying it to the source too (using the source's own unchanged tracking id): guidekn/empty sources rebuild canonically, custom URLs get `sub_id3` rewritten. Now all N variants come out identically shaped, matching a normal stage where the tracking-ID chip was clicked. No schema change; sends unaffected (sub_id3 == tracking_id, `validateDestination` passes). Verified [scripts/test-split-url-rebuild.ts](../scripts/test-split-url-rebuild.ts) (added variant-A cases) + tsc clean — updated 04-features/campaigns-stages-creatives, CHANGELOG.

## 2026-07-20 — Hourly report: added a **Sent** first column + **% rates** per action. Sent is bucketed by SEND hour (`stage_sends.sent_at`, tracked; manual sends roll into the Manual row via `sms_count`); engagement stays activity-time. Rates use the same formulas as the other tabs (OptOut%/CR = ÷ sent, Redir% = ÷ clickers, Sales CR = ÷ redirects) — since Sent is send-time and engagement is activity-time, a per-hour rate is a cross-cohort ratio (an hour with clicks but no sends shows 0%). Columns: Sent · Opt-outs+% · Clickers+CR% · Redirects+Redir% · Sales+Sales CR · Revenue (no cost/EPC/profit — cost is a per-stage lump). [lib/reporting/performance-report.ts](../lib/reporting/performance-report.ts) + [components/reports/performance-report.tsx](../components/reports/performance-report.tsx). Verified test-performance-report.ts + tsc + next build — updated 04-features/reports-rollup, CHANGELOG.

## 2026-07-20 — Hourly report now takes a date RANGE (was single-day). Each hour-of-day (0–23) is summed across every day in the selected from/to range, so operators see the aggregate engagement curve over a week/month, not one day. Change: `getHourlyReport()` window ends at `to` (not `from`) and groups by `EXTRACT(HOUR …)` across the range ([lib/reporting/performance-report.ts](../lib/reporting/performance-report.ts)); route stops clamping `to=from` for hourly; UI shows From/To pickers on the hourly tab. Manual row rolls up manual results over the range. Verified scripts/test-performance-report.ts (5-day range aggregates) + tsc + next build — updated 04-features/reports-rollup, CHANGELOG.

## 2026-07-20 — Reports re-sourced to MATCH the Overview (Keitaro) tab. The five `/reports` tabs read ~7% low on first live use because they used the per-recipient/internal-clicks basis approved in recon; operator wants Overview parity. Extracted the Overview route's per-stage funnel into a shared [lib/reporting/stage-funnel.ts](../lib/reporting/stage-funnel.ts) `getStageMetricsInRange()` and made the Overview route ([app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts](../app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts)) call it — so they can't drift. By Number/Offer/Sequence now group those exact per-stage numbers → equal Overview to the cent. By Group distributes each stage's Overview total across the campaign's used contact groups (tracked: per-contact ⅟k weights from clicks/conversions/opt-outs; manual: audience-allocation share; equal-split fallback) → group rows reconcile to the stage total (≤2 decimals). Hourly re-sourced to **user-activity time** (internal event tables: clicks.clicked_at, converted_at, offer_reached_at, opt_out_attributions.created_at) with a pinned **Manual** row — deliberately NOT Overview's Keitaro click count. EPC fixed to revenue÷redirects (Overview's formula). The Phase-1 rollup (report_stage_hour/report_group_hour, report-rollup cron, stage_sends snapshot cols) is now UNUSED by reports — retained, flagged for a retirement migration. Verified: scripts/test-stage-funnel.ts (Overview parity to the cent) + scripts/test-performance-report.ts (all dims reconcile) + tsc + next build. No migration — updated 04-features/reports-rollup, CHANGELOG.

## 2026-07-20 — Manual TextHub opt-out import (scroll-off recovery). New one-shot [scripts/import-texthub-optouts.ts](../scripts/import-texthub-optouts.ts) imports STOP replies the poller missed (200-cap inbox scroll-off) from a TextHub inbox export (`From,Received`): creates the missing org-wide `opt_outs` (`source='texthub_manual_import'`), attributes each with the live single-most-recent-send rule (`DISTINCT ON (oo.id)`, not the older backfill's fan-out), recomputes stage counters/cost, and refreshes the reports rollup. Dry-run by default (rolled-back tx); `--apply` commits. Idempotent (dedup window + `ON CONFLICT`). Auto-detects the export timezone by calibrating candidate zones against stored opt-outs — the export's `Received` is Mountain wall-clock, NOT Eastern despite spreadsheet labels. First run recovered 8,523 opt-outs / 6,983 attributions across 146 stages for Jul 1–20 2026. Spec: [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-20-texthub-optout-manual-import-design.md](superpowers/specs/2026-07-20-texthub-optout-manual-import-design.md). No migration/schema change. Updated 04-features/crons.md.

## 2026-07-19 — Reports rollup Phase 2 (read API + UI). The five performance reports are now visible as tabs under `/reports`: Overview (the existing Keitaro funnel, moved verbatim to [components/reports/keitaro-report.tsx](../components/reports/keitaro-report.tsx)) + By Number / By Offer / By Sequence / Hourly / By Group, each a `/reports/<dimension>` child route ([app/(protected)/reports/[dimension]/page.tsx](../app/(protected)/reports/[dimension]/page.tsx) → [components/reports/performance-report.tsx](../components/reports/performance-report.tsx)) under a shared tabbed layout. Read API `GET /api/reports/performance` (gated `campaigns.view`) reads the rollup via [lib/reporting/performance-report.ts](../lib/reporting/performance-report.ts); client-safe dimension constants split into [lib/reporting/report-dimensions.ts](../lib/reporting/report-dimensions.ts). New sidebar **Reports** group (nav `isActive` gained an `exact` flag). Shared [`<ProviderPhoneCell>`](../components/provider-phone-cell.tsx) extracted from the campaigns column and reused by the By-Number report + the campaigns list. Default range = today ET (hourly = single day); EPC/profit/% derived at read; provider/number filter across all tabs; totals always from Fact A (group rows fan out, footnoted); per-recipient-sales reconciliation footnote. No migration. Verified: tsc + `next build` + [scripts/test-performance-report.ts](../scripts/test-performance-report.ts) (all five dimensions vs live data) — updated 04-features/reports-rollup, CHANGELOG. NOT yet deployed (feature branch feat/reports-ui).

## 2026-07-19 — Opt-out poll cadence `*/15` → every 5 min (200-cap scroll-off mitigation) — docs: 04-features/crons, 05-flows, 06-integrations, CHANGELOG
- TextHub's `?inbox=true` is hard-capped at the 200 most-recent messages with no pagination, so during a STOP-reply burst more than 200 replies could accumulate between 15-min polls and scroll off before ingest (~30% opt-out loss Jun 29–Jul 5; 46% Jul 4 peak — see the 2026-07-06 backfill entry). Tightened `/api/opt-outs/poll` in [vercel.json](../vercel.json) from `6,21,36,51 * * * *` to `1,6,11,16,21,26,31,36,41,46,51,56 * * * *` (every 5 min, staggered onto residue-1 minutes so it avoids the two heavy `*/5` jobs `send-scheduled`/`keitaro-poll` and every 15-min cron, overlapping only the lighter `lookup-worker` on alternating ticks). Shrinks the accumulation window 3× — a **mitigation, not a cure** (a >200 burst inside 5 min still overflows; true fixes remain pagination TextHub doesn't offer, or CSV backfill). No code change: the route already guards overlap via `withCronLease("opt-outs-poll")`, the inbox read is free, and `maxDuration = 60` ≪ the 5-min interval. Reported to TextHub support in parallel.

## 2026-07-19 — Reports rollup Phase 1 (data layer). Migration `0112`: two hourly-bucket fact tables (`report_stage_hour`, `report_group_hour`) feeding five reports (by number / offer / sequence message / hourly / group) over one shared metric set, plus durable per-send snapshot columns `stage_sends.provider_phone_id` + `cost_per_sms` (stamped at materialization in [lib/sends/kickoff.ts](../lib/sends/kickoff.ts)). Maintenance is a bounded 14-day rolling-window UPSERT ([lib/reporting/rollup.ts](../lib/reporting/rollup.ts)) under `withCronLease`, cron `/api/cron/report-rollup` at `14,29,44,59`. Bucketing by the SEND hour in ET; EPC/profit/rates derived at read time. Sales/revenue from per-recipient `stage_sends` (~93% of the Keitaro aggregate, reconciliation delta surfaced); clickers = internal clean clicks (≠ Keitaro visits); `report_group_hour` fans out over contact groups (non-additive — totals come from Fact A). Backfill [scripts/backfill-report-rollup.ts](../scripts/backfill-report-rollup.ts) is preflight-only by default. **Read views / API / UI are a later phase.** Verified read-only against live data ([scripts/test-report-rollup.ts](../scripts/test-report-rollup.ts): rows=302, sent=967,281, sales=295, rev=$20,982, Fact B=2,024). Migration NOT yet applied to prod (gated on review). Recon: [REPORTS-ROLLUP-RECON.md](../REPORTS-ROLLUP-RECON.md) — updated 03-data-model, 04-features/reports-rollup (new), 04-features/crons, 05-flows, 07-conventions

## 2026-07-18 — Guidekn destination-shape guard: allow digit-bearing slugs. The canonical slug pattern was `[a-z]+` (letters only), so a valid destination like `https://www.guidekn.com/lp/gb1?sub_id3=…` false-tripped the "tracking ID concatenated into the path" block in the stage form (and would have failed the DB CHECK at send time). Fix: `validateDestination`/`GUIDEKN_DEST_RE` in [lib/stage-url.ts](../lib/stage-url.ts) widen the slug to `[a-z0-9]+`, and Defect A now keys off an UNDERSCORE in the `/lp/` path segment (the reliable signature of a glued tracking id — tracking ids always contain underscores) instead of a trailing digit. Migration 0111 widens the `link_destinations_guidekn_url_shape` CHECK to match (still `NOT VALID`). Concat bug still caught (`…/lp/gb18_80_…` rejected). Verified: scripts/test-validate-destination.ts (incl. new gb1/gb18 accept + digit-slug concat reject) — updated 03-data-model, 07-conventions, 04-features/tracking-attribution

## 2026-07-17 — Move the stage cancel action out of the "Send…" panel onto the stage row's ⋯ menu ("Cancel send"), for discoverability. One consolidated item replaces both former in-panel cancel buttons ("Cancel prepared send" + the send-now "Cancel"); it's gated on the same server-guard condition (materialized + pending remainder + nothing sent/sending) and opens a confirm dialog showing the pending count. Server endpoint (`…/send/abort`) unchanged. Panel's Prepared box now points at the ⋯ menu — updated 04-features/sms-send-pipeline

## 2026-07-17 — Cancel a materialized stage before send (revert-to-editable). Completed the existing `…/send/abort` recall into a re-materializable cancel: it now also resets `campaign_stages.materialized_at = NULL`; recipient re-enumeration excludes `rejected` rows (`status <> 'rejected'` in lib/sends/recipients.ts) so canceled contacts re-materialize cleanly; the panel status count excludes `rejected` from `total` so the stage flips back to editable. Exposed a "Cancel" button on the materialized send-now branch (previously only "Cancel prepared send"). Confirmed `rejected` is written ONLY by the abort/cancel path — no delivery/provider path writes it (delivery rejections land as `failed`/`filtered`, which still block re-enumeration), so re-materialize can't re-text a genuinely-rejected contact. Sibling count fix (required for correctness): the stages-list and `/sends/today` `total` queries now also exclude `rejected` and no longer fold it into `failed` — otherwise a canceled stage (materialized_at NULL + rejected rows) would be stuck reading Indigo "Materializing" via deriveStageOperationalStatus, and re-materialized stages would double-count. No migration (reused `rejected`); scheduled_at kept; orphan links left as audit. Guard unchanged (refuses once anything sent/sending). Verified via scripts/test-cancel-rematerialize.ts (21/21, incl. operational-status derivation) — updated 04-features/sms-send-pipeline, 04-features/daily-volume-ui

## 2026-07-17 — Scope drain rolling volume ceilings (`max_sends_per_minute` / `_24h`) PER PROVIDER — `countSentSince` now joins `stage_sends → campaign_stages.sms_provider_id` instead of counting org-wide, so one provider's volume no longer trips another's ceiling (incident: a due Ahoi stage stalled forever on `rate_24h` while ~30k TextHub sends exceeded Ahoi's 10k default; ClickUp 869e659t4). No migration, no new index. Regression test added to `scripts/verify-drain.ts` — updated 04-features/sms-send-pipeline, 04-features/daily-volume-ui

## 2026-07-14 — W1.1: decouple clicker propagation into its own cron — docs/04-features/crons
- New cron `/api/cron/propagate-clickers` (`8,23,38,53 * * * *`) runs `propagateTrackedClickers()` under `withCronLease("propagate-clickers", …)`; the inline best-effort call was removed from `/api/clicks/score-pending` (now scoring-only). Fixes a starvation bug: a heavy scoring run ate score-pending's 60 s budget and never reached the tail propagate, stalling the watermark ~5 h on 2026-07-14 while clicks kept scoring. The `propagate-clickers` `cron_locks` row is shared by design — `lease_until` (lease) + `watermark` (progress) are distinct columns. No schema change (reuses migration 0103's `cron_locks`). New file: `app/api/cron/propagate-clickers/route.ts`.

## 2026-07-14 — Targeted (scoped) lookups: group action + upload-existing list — docs/04-features/phone-lookup-carrier, docs/07-conventions
- Two manager-gated entry points into the EXISTING lookup queue/worker/cap/lease/balance machinery. (A) "Look up this group" — per-row action on the Stats Panel (`GET group-preview` + `POST enqueue-group`); `enqueueGroup` is a set-based `INSERT..SELECT` from the group join (no phone round-trip at 100K+ scale) with the same cache-complete/already-pending guards. (B) "Look up a list of existing numbers" — new `/settings/lookup` card (`POST match-preview` + `POST enqueue-matched`); matches a pasted/CSV list against existing contacts ONLY, never creates contacts, re-matches server-side on enqueue. Only-missing (no re-lookup path); 25k large-run type-to-confirm; provisional cost (real spend on the Est-vs-Billed line). No schema change — scoped runs reuse `trigger='upload'` (the trigger CHECK is unchanged). New files: `lib/telnyx/match-list.ts`, four routes under `app/api/telnyx/lookup/`. Verified read-only against prod: preview.remaining == Stats Panel predicate (148,013) and the enqueueGroup query (rolled back) produces exactly to_enqueue rows; match splits real vs fabricated correctly and creates no contacts; gating rejects operator/viewer.

Trimmed the backfill-only assertions out of `scripts/test-lookup-uploads.ts` (its csv_import/preview coverage stays). No schema change; shared enqueue core, worker, daily cap, lease + balance gates untouched. Lookups are now always scoped (upload / group / matched list). Isolated commit ahead of the targeted-lookups build.

## 2026-07-14 — Stage editor density + sticky top action bar + layout void fix (presentation-only) — docs/04-features/campaigns-stages-creatives
- `stage-form.tsx`/`stage-inline-creator.tsx`: compact spacing pass; added a sticky top Save/Cancel bar sharing one action context with the bottom bar; moved Notes to the bottom of the left column so Results sits higher in the right rail. Fixed the persistent inter-section blank space — the tall right rail was stretching the main column and `align-content:stretch` (grid default) spread the slack as gaps between sections; fixed with `items-start` on the body grid + `content-start` on the main column. Editor `<Card>` set to `overflow-visible` so the sticky bar isn't clipped. No behavior/data/query changes; locked/sent-stage semantics untouched.

## 2026-07-14 — Fix: chunk the bulk-CSV client upload (413 on large files) — code only
- `BulkUpdateSection` (`/settings/lookup`) POSTed the whole parsed CSV in one request; a 114K-row file (~7MB) exceeded Vercel's ~4.5MB serverless request-body limit → 413 (and the route's 100K-row Zod cap). Now the client POSTs in 20K-row chunks (~1.5MB each), aggregates the per-chunk `CsvImportResult`, and shows "Uploading N / M rows…" progress. Idempotent upsert → a mid-run failure is safe to retry. Pairs with the earlier server-side DB-param chunking (both are needed: this is the HTTP body limit, that was the Postgres bind-param limit).

## 2026-07-14 — Fix: carrier AI triage self-heals after a global API outage — code only
- The triage cron incremented each string's `attempts` on a GLOBAL API failure (an Anthropic "organization disabled" 400 disabled every call), so after 3 runs all 76 queued strings were stranded `pending` (unclaimable at `attempts < 3`) even after the org was re-enabled. Now a batch-level API/transport failure records `last_error` but does NOT consume the per-string attempt budget (`recordBatchError` in `lib/carrier/ai-triage.ts`) — rows stay pending and auto-retry once the API recovers. Per-string verdict failures still park via `markNeedsHuman` (which bumps attempts). Telegram alert still fires each failing run for visibility.

## 2026-07-14 — Lookup Stats Panel: coverage + landline suppression per contact group (migration 0106) — docs/04-features/phone-lookup-carrier, docs/03-data-model
- New read-only panel on `/settings/lookup` (`LookupStatsSection`) showing per-contact-group lookup coverage (Telnyx vs manual, from `phone_lookups.source` — no migration needed, it already existed), landline suppression, sendable (reuses the audience builder's `messaging_status='eligible'` + `opt_outs` anti-join verbatim), and remaining-un-looked-up (>50% highlighted). Columns are a disjoint partition (total = sendable + landlines + non-landline opt-outs) enforced by a PERMANENT assertion in `computeLookupGroupStats` (`lib/telnyx/lookup-stats.ts`) that throws on any future drift. Summary strip = distinct contacts (multi-group deduped). NO cost/spend metric (deferred).
- The ~2s full-population aggregate is cached in `lookup_group_stats_cache` (migration 0106, additive/reversible): panel reads it in ~46ms with a prominent "as of ET" + "may be stale" (>15min) label; manager-only "Refresh now" recomputes. Fail-safe: compute runs before the atomic single-row upsert, so a failed refresh leaves the prior cache intact (verified). `GET /api/telnyx/lookup/group-stats` + `POST …/refresh` (manager+). Tests: `scripts/test-lookup-stats.ts`. NOT deployed (branch `feat/lookup-stats-panel`); 0106 applied to prod for verification.

## 2026-07-14 — Fix: chunk the CSV bulk-update upsert (carrier lookup) — code only
- `importCsvLookups` (`lib/telnyx/csv-import.ts`) sent every row in one INSERT; at scale (a 36K-row bulk carrier CSV) that blew Postgres's 65,535-bind-parameter limit → 500 on `POST /api/telnyx/lookup/csv-update`. Pre-existing scaling bug (not the v2 flip). Now chunks the upsert AND the contact-sync at 3,000 rows/batch (idempotent upsert, safe per-batch). Confirmed via rolled-back reproduction (20K single insert fails; chunked succeeds).

## 2026-07-13 — Carrier normalization v2: resolver chain + AI triage (migrations 0104–0105) — docs/04-features/phone-lookup-carrier, docs/03-data-model, docs/04-features/crons, docs/06-integrations
- `carrier_norm` now resolves through a single shared classifier (`lib/carrier/classify.ts`): Telnyx `normalized_carrier` → learned `carrier_mappings` (normalized key) → seeded `carrier_patterns` regex → `Unmapped`→AI triage. Gated by `lookup_settings.carrier_resolver_v2` (default false); buckets unchanged. Key normalization strips route/SPID suffixes so variants collapse to one mapping.
- New tables `carrier_patterns` (0104), `carrier_classify_queue` (0105); `phone_lookups.normalized_carrier` + `lookup_settings.carrier_resolver_v2`/`carrier_ai_run_cap`. New cron `/api/cron/carrier-triage` (`17,47`) batches unresolved strings to `claude-haiku-4-5` (constrained JSON), writes confident buckets to `carrier_mappings`, parks the rest for human review (`/settings/lookup` triage section, ranked by contact count). New dep `@anthropic-ai/sdk`; env `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`. Backfill: `scripts/backfill-carrier-v2.ts` (dry-run default, `--apply` with rollback snapshot). Tests: `scripts/test-carrier-classify.ts`. NOT YET APPLIED to prod; flag OFF pending backfill review.

No conflicting `preferredRegion`/`runtime` exports existed beforehand.

## 2026-07-10 — Telnyx Number Lookup: 500-number calibration + batch spend reconciliation (migration 0102) — docs/04-features/phone-lookup-carrier
- Ran the 500-number calibration through the backfill path: 500/500, 0 failed; mix 93% mobile / 5% landline / 1% voip / 1% unknown; 24 landlines flipped to `not_applicable` (17 historical `sent` rows untouched); 10 unmapped strings, all bucketed via `assignCarrierMapping` (`lib/telnyx/assign-mapping.ts`, `mapped_by='admin'`, retroactive) → 0 Unmapped remaining. Deferred EXPLAIN re-check: partial indexes chosen naturally for selective queries (`contacts_org_created_eligible_idx`, `contacts_org_carrier_eligible_idx`); is_not universe still seqscans (only 24 not_applicable) but usable.

Migration 0102 adds `lookup_batches.balance_before_usd`/`balance_after_usd`; the worker snapshots balance before first drain + at finalize, and the batch Telegram summary now reads `Est (rate): $X · Billed (ledger): $Y` (balance delta = truth). `lookup_rate_mobile` set to **0 (PROVISIONAL** — inferred from balance delta + absence of Mcc/Mnc lines in the not-yet-posted Telnyx Usage Report, not confirmed). Full-backfill preflight: re-check the Usage Report (Lrn + Mcc/Mnc) on the calibration date; if Mcc/Mnc ~466 records, restore 0.0025 + re-quote before running.

## 2026-07-10 — Telnyx Number Lookup, phase 5 (backend): upload preview/enqueue, backfill, csv-update — docs/04-features/phone-lookup-carrier
- New `/api/telnyx/lookup/` routes + `lib/telnyx/` helpers: `preview` (review-panel counts/cost/balance, read-only), `enqueue` (upload numbers, dedup — decoupled from per-entity upload routes so one endpoint covers every phone-upload path), `backfill` + `backfill/preview` (distinct non-archived phones needing lookup, archived-excluded, cost, balance, daily-cap ETA; optional `sampleLimit` **randomly** sampled — the 500-number calibration uses this exact path), `csv-update` (bulk-update existing from predefined line_type/carrier). Permissions added: `lookup.run` (operator+), `lookup.admin` (manager+).
- Reconfirmed + tested (`scripts/test-lookup-uploads.ts`): `csv_import` never overwrites a `telnyx` row; type-without-carrier → `Unknown`; landline → `Unknown`; garbage `line_type` → `unknown`; same-file dupes collapse; predefined rows excluded from enqueue (no double spend); toggle OFF → `Unidentified` default (no enqueue). UI wiring (toggle + review panel in `PhoneUploadForm`, backfill admin page) is phase 5b.

## 2026-07-10 — Telnyx Number Lookup, phase 4: eligible-invariant wiring (landlines absent from audiences) — docs/04-features/phone-lookup-carrier
- `AND messaging_status = 'eligible'` threaded in as a SQL **literal** (not a bind — required for the planner to match the partial indexes) at the two per-dimension builders every consumer shares: `buildSegmentAudienceClause` (output `gateEligible` join — catches manual members + non-contacts rules; `is_not` universe + `contact_added_*` scans carry the literal → use `contacts_org_eligible_idx` / `contacts_org_created_eligible_idx`) and `buildGroupMembershipClause` (eligible inner-join). So preview, snapshot, and draft counts share one landline-free audience.
- Send backstop in `enumerateStageRecipients` ([lib/sends/recipients.ts](../../lib/sends/recipients.ts)) is **telemetry**, not a silent filter: a leaked `not_applicable` recipient is logged + skipped + counted + Telegram-alerted (checklist #14). Active-pool reads unchanged (landlines never enter the pool).
- Verified `scripts/test-eligible-gate.ts`: a landline drops out of the segment audience but stays in contacts (#3); partial-index selection confirmed via `EXPLAIN` (+ `enable_seqscan=off` to prove the literal matches). **Deferred:** re-run `EXPLAIN` after the calibration batch + backfill once `not_applicable` rows exist.

## 2026-07-10 — Telnyx Number Lookup, phase 3: the lookup worker + `*/2` cron (migrations 0100–0101) — docs/04-features/crons, phone-lookup-carrier, 06-integrations
- `lib/telnyx/` worker: `enqueueLookups` (batch + dedup against cache-complete/pending) and `runLookupWorker` (cron `/api/cron/lookup-worker`, `*/2`, `maxDuration=300`, CRON_SECRET). Contact-sync helper built here for end-to-end (copies line_type/carrier to contacts across orgs replacing `Unidentified`; landline → cancel PENDING `stage_sends` only + remove from `campaign_audience_pool`).
- **Single-runner lease** (migration 0100 `lookup_settings.worker_lease_until`) — a lease row, NOT `pg_try_advisory_lock` (unsafe through the transaction pooler): conditional-UPDATE claim, 4-min lease, CAS heartbeat-renew, clear on exit; overlapping invocation no-ops, crashed drain's lease expires and the next tick proceeds.
- **Daily cap counts attempts** (SUM `lookup_queue.attempts` since Warsaw midnight; migration 0100 adds `lookup_queue.updated_at`) so 429s/retries consume cap (429×2+success = 3, not 1). Guards ordered paused → cap → balance (402/feature-gate ⇒ halt+alert, low balance ⇒ alert+skip+auto-resume). Claim `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` (attempts++ at claim), 429 → 60s cooldown backoff, `rps` pacing, Telegram batch summary.
- Migration 0101: `contacts_phone_number_idx` btree (built CONCURRENTLY) — cross-org phone equality for the cache→contacts sync (the unique `(org_id, phone_number)` can't serve a bare `phone_number =`).
- Verified without Telnyx HTTP: lease overlap/CAS/crash-recovery, attempt-summed cap, enqueue dedup (`scripts/test-lookup-worker.ts`). Migrations 0100–0101 applied to the shared DB; integrity 101/101. Live-fire drain gated on `TELNYX_API_KEY` (first run = 500-number calibration batch).

## 2026-07-10 — Telnyx Number Lookup: split carrier_norm `Unknown` into `Unidentified` + `Unknown` (migration 0099) — docs/04-features/phone-lookup-carrier, 03-data-model, 07-conventions
- Semantic correction before Phase 4/6 wiring (so no rework): `Unidentified` = no `phone_lookups` row exists (never looked up) — the new `contacts.carrier_norm` default; `Unknown` = a lookup occurred but the carrier is undetermined. `Unmapped` now groups with `Unknown` (looked-up family), not with `Unidentified`. Invariant: `carrier_norm='Unidentified'` ⇔ no lookup row; `phone_lookups.carrier_norm` may never be `Unidentified` (already enforced by the 0095 CHECK — verified rejected).
- Migration 0099: `contacts.carrier_norm` default `Unknown`→`Unidentified`; guarded one-time flip of existing rows (only while `phone_lookups` empty — verified, all 752,707 flipped via 50k batches, no long lock); adds `contacts_carrier_norm_check` (six buckets + `Unmapped` + `Unidentified`). Applied + integrity 100/100 green.
- Filter/rule/reporting semantics recorded: campaign filter excludes `Unidentified` whenever any filter is set (own reported line), `Unidentified` not selectable there; segment rules offer both; reporting counts them separately. (Wiring lands in Phases 4–6.)

## 2026-07-10 — Telnyx Number Lookup, phase 1 (schema): carrier/line-type enrichment + landline suppression foundation (migrations 0095–0098) — docs/04-features/phone-lookup-carrier (new), 03-data-model, 06-integrations, 07-conventions
- New feature in build on branch `feat/telnyx-number-lookup`: phone line-type + carrier enrichment via the Telnyx Number Lookup API, with a DB-enforced landline hard stop, carrier-based segmentation, and campaign-level carrier exclusion. This entry covers the phase-1 schema only; worker/upload/UI land in later phases.
- **Global cache** `phone_lookups` (migration 0095, no `org_id` — carrier is a fact about a number): PK `phone` (E.164 `+1XXXXXXXXXX`, matches `contacts.phone_number` — verified all 752,707 rows conform), `line_type`/`carrier_raw`/`carrier_norm`/`ocn`/`spid`/`ported`/`source`/`lookup_status`/`raw_response`. Plus `carrier_mappings` (raw string → one of six buckets, seeded) and account-global `lookup_settings` (single-row: pause switch, daily cap, editable rates, concurrency). All three RLS-enabled **policy-less** (geoip_cache/0066 pattern — server-only, no org to scope).
- **`contacts` enrichment** (migration 0096): `line_type` + `carrier_norm` (denormalized) + `messaging_status` (`eligible`/`not_applicable`). The landline hard stop is a **trigger** (`contacts_derive_messaging_status`), not a generated column, to avoid a full-table ACCESS EXCLUSIVE rewrite at scale — ADD COLUMNs are metadata-only (constant defaults), no backfill needed. Four eligible-partial indexes (`WHERE messaging_status='eligible'`) built concurrently in prod via [scripts/apply-eligible-indexes-concurrent.ts](../../scripts/apply-eligible-indexes-concurrent.ts) (0088 pattern). Send-record carrier stamp `stage_sends.carrier_norm` (future per-carrier analytics enabler).
- **Batch/queue** `lookup_batches` (org-scoped SELECT RLS) + `lookup_queue` (policy-less) (migration 0097). **Segment rule types** `phone_type` + `carrier` added to the `segment_rules_rule_type_check` (migration 0098).
- Telnyx API notes (verified vs the `telnyx-node` SDK): `carrier.type` has no `landline` value — map `fixed line`→landline, prefer `portability.line_type`, exotic/ambiguous→unknown (stays eligible); `/v2/balance` fields are strings; mobile-only pricing surcharge. Env: `TELNYX_API_KEY` (+ optional `TELNYX_API_URL`).

## 2026-07-10 — Guidekn destination-URL shape guard: block malformed tracking links at 4 layers (migration 0094) — docs/04-features/tracking-attribution, 04-features/campaigns-stages-creatives, 03-data-model, 07-conventions
- A string-concatenation bug in the stage form's tracking-ID chip (`appendRawValue` glued the id as a bare value) produced malformed guidekn destinations — id in the path (`…/lp/knd8_62_…`), empty `sub_id3=`, or an unsubstituted `subid3=sub_id3` placeholder — each a 404 that silently killed attribution (07/09 Daily Report: 2 stages / ~10.7K sends with zero Keitaro tracking).
- New `validateDestination(url, trackingId?)` ([lib/stage-url.ts](../../lib/stage-url.ts)) is the single source of truth, wired into: (1) the stage form (Save disabled + specific defect named; tracking-ID chip now uses `setUrlParam` to attach a proper `sub_id3=<id>`), (2) stage `POST`/`PATCH` (4xx on `full_url`), (3) the send path ([lib/sends/kickoff.ts](../../lib/sends/kickoff.ts) — trusts stored `full_url` only when well-formed, else rebuilds; refuses a malformed resolved destination with `invalid_destination`; replaced the `includes()` check fooled by id-in-path), (4) DB CHECK `link_destinations_guidekn_url_shape` (migration 0094, `NOT VALID`).
- Split/behavioral routes now rebuild each sibling/lane `full_url` **canonically from its own tracking id** (guidekn/empty sources) instead of inheriting-and-patching a malformed base; custom non-guidekn URLs preserved. Split dialogs preview each variant's tracking id **live** (parity with a regular stage's on-the-go preview).
- Backfill: [scripts/backfill-guidekn-destinations.ts](../../scripts/backfill-guidekn-destinations.ts) repaired 12 of 13 legacy malformed rows (dest URL + stale/empty `full_url`); `stage_id=516` (tracking_id `8_62_061226_3_s6_c124`, `full_url` was a `clicks2scale.com` network URL) left un-repaired by request → constraint added `NOT VALID`, not yet `VALIDATE`d. Tests: `scripts/test-validate-destination.ts` (all §6 cases), `scripts/test-split-url-rebuild.ts` (drift/renumber).

## 2026-07-10 — Reports now includes stages sent in-range with no Keitaro row (Cost/Total Sent reconcile with Telegram) — docs/04-features/keitaro-poll
- `GET /api/keitaro/reports` folded in only stages that had a `keitaro_stage_results` row, so a stage sent in-range that got zero tracked clicks (or wasn't polled yet) was invisible — its send Cost and Total Sent were silently dropped, making the Reports totals under-report vs reality and vs the Telegram spend snapshot (07/09: Reports $176.50 vs Telegram $288.97; the gap was 3 tracked stages / $112.47 with no Keitaro row).
- Fix ([app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts](../../app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts)): after building the Keitaro fold, query `campaign_stages` sent within `[from, day-after-to)` (org-scoped, `archived_at IS NULL`) and seed an empty funnel for any not already present. The existing per-stage phase fills their `total_sent` / `opt_outs` / `cost` unchanged. Additive + read-only; Keitaro-present stages are untouched (`byStage.has` guard).

## 2026-07-10 — "Prepared for today" total added to the /sends/today volume card — docs/04-features/daily-volume-ui
- The volume card on `/sends/today` gained a "Prepared for today" line above the "Sent today" meter: the total messages materialized (`Σ counts.total`) across every tracked stage in play today. Derived client-side from the existing `GET /api/sends/today` payload (no API/schema change); accumulates as stages are prepared.

## 2026-07-09 — Campaign audience preview now reflects the "Exclude leads who already got this offer" toggle (content-dedup LAYER 3, preview-only) — docs/04-features/content-dedup, 04-features/audience-snapshot
- `previewAudience` gained `excludePriorOffer` + `offerId` inputs and a `got_offer_in_prior_campaign` output; when the toggle is on the preview subtracts LAYER 3 from `total_matching` and the campaign form shows a "N leads excluded — already received this offer" line. The frozen snapshot is unchanged (send-time anti-join stays the gate); the count is a point-in-time estimate.
- Perf-preserving: with the toggle off there is no `oe_set` CTE, join, or `offer_exposures` access (only constant `false` literals that fold away); `flagSetCtes`/`flagJoins` take an optional offer id and are byte-for-byte unchanged for the snapshot/draft callers. Wired through `audiencePreviewSchema` (`offer_id`), the audience-preview route, and the campaign form (request body + debounce deps + new line).

## 2026-07-08 — Added per-offer Group Performance Report (matviews + twice-daily cron) — docs/03-data-model, 04-features/offer-group-report, 04-features/crons, 06-integrations, 07-conventions
- New `/offers/[id]/report` page + `GET /api/offers/[id]/report`: per-(offer, contact group) lifetime economics (Sends/RPM/Net RPM/EPC/Sales/Opt-out %/Net profit) plus list-pressure (`Sent 7d/30d/90d`) and `Fresh pool`, with a pinned org-wide benchmark row and an offer-total row. Both tracked and manual campaigns are included in the economics; only per-contact columns are limited to in-app sends (any `link_mode`).
- Precomputed via migration `0093_offer_group_report` (plain view `offer_report_campaign_econ` + materialized views `offer_group_report_mv`/`offer_report_org_summary_mv` + `report_refresh_log` + supporting indexes), refreshed twice daily by a new Vercel cron `/api/cron/refresh-offer-group-report` (`0 5,20 * * *`, `CRON_SECRET`-gated, `maxDuration=300`).
- Multi-group campaigns count fully in each targeted group (group rows can sum to more than the de-duplicated org benchmark) — footnoted in the UI, documented in [07-conventions.md](07-conventions.md).

## 2026-07-07 — Partial send failures no longer fail the whole stage or zero its cost — docs: 04-features/sms-send-pipeline, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- **Symptom (campaign `8_62_070726_2`):** an A/B stage sent 2,500/2,529 but 29 were stranded in `sending` (interrupted drain); the stage read Red "Failed" and its cost showed **$0** despite the sends. **Root cause:** cost was gated on `campaign_stages.sent_at`, which stays NULL when a drain is cut off (300s cap / crash) before its finalization step; and the derived status flipped to Red on ANY `sending`/`failed` row. The stage had **0 `pending` rows**, so the drain (which only selects stages with pending rows) could never re-reach it to heal.
- **Fixes (no schema change):** (A) `total-cost.ts` bills from `status='sent'` rows, not `sent_at` — a fully-sent stage always shows its cost. (B) `stage-status.ts` `deriveStageOperationalStatus` reads Green "Sent" whenever anything sent; failed/stuck/dedup-skipped rows become a **warning** (`stageSendWarningCount`), and Red is reserved for a missed window or a terminal stage with **nothing** sent. (C+D) new `lib/sends/reconcile-stages.ts` `reconcileStuckStages`, called each `send-scheduled` cron tick: finalizes stranded stages — marks stale `sending` (idle >15 min, so never a live drain) → `failed` **without re-sending** (at-most-once — a stuck row may already be delivered; double-texting is worse), stamps `sent_at`, recomputes cost.
- Verified: `test-stage-status` (10), `test-total-cost-gate` (3), `test-reconcile-stages` (9). Once deployed, the cron heals stage 740 on its next tick (cost → **$25.97**, status → Green + "29 not delivered").

## 2026-07-07 — Telegram hourly report: fix silent cold-start hang + build-phase blind spot — docs: 04-features/crons, CHANGELOG
- **Symptom:** the hourly performance report stopped arriving for days while the **daily** report kept coming — and no `⚠️ failed` alert fired either (total silence). **Root cause (two compounding).** (1) `buildHourly` ([app/api/cron/telegram-report/route.ts](../app/api/cron/telegram-report/route.ts)) ran `2× computeReportMetrics` via `Promise.all` = **8 concurrent queries**; on a cold serverless start during busy ET hours the concurrent connection acquisition stalled the pooler past `maxDuration=60` and the function was killed. Queries are ~16 ms each — the cost was cold concurrent *connections*, not execution — so it only bit the heavier hourly path (daily fires 4 queries at the quiet 05:00 ET hour and never tripped it). (2) The metrics **build ran outside the send try/catch**, so that kill produced no report *and* no alert. Diagnosed live: a plain hourly call hung 40 s+ (vs 1.9 s warm) while the DB sat idle and `salesRevenueTotals` EXPLAIN-ANALYZE'd at 16 ms.
- **Fix.** Hourly now fetches today's full metrics (4 queries) + yesterday's **spend only** (`spendInRange`, newly exported from [lib/reporting/report-snapshot.ts](../lib/reporting/report-snapshot.ts)) **sequentially** — 8→5 queries, peak concurrency 8→4 (matches daily). Build + send are wrapped in one try/catch under a **50 s `withTimeout`** (below `maxDuration`), so a hung build now returns 500 **and** fires the `notifyTelegram` `⚠️` alert instead of dying silently. Builds on the 2026-07-03 retry/alert hardening (`d6abfe8`).

## 2026-07-07 — Fix "Could not preview audience" on the stage form (timeout mis-detection) — docs: 04-features/content-dedup, CHANGELOG
- **Symptom:** the stage form's STAGE AUDIENCE block failed entirely with "Could not preview audience.", blocking the whole panel. **Root cause:** `computeStageEligibilityPreview` ([lib/audience-snapshot.ts](../lib/audience-snapshot.ts)) is meant to catch a `SET LOCAL statement_timeout` cancellation (SQLSTATE `57014`) and degrade to a `truncated` preview, but it checked `err.message` — and postgres-js via drizzle puts the `57014` code and "statement timeout" text on `err.cause`, not `err.message` (the message is `"Failed query: …"`). So a legitimate timeout re-threw → the route 500'd → even the working audience count (which the UI already renders alongside a "too large to compute the dedup preview" note) was lost. Surfaced "recently" because the eligibility query only crosses the 10s limit intermittently (cold serverless connections + grown exposure/opt-out ledgers). Reproduced against a real campaign: a cold-connection run cancelled with `57014` and re-threw.
- **Fix:** new [`isStatementTimeout`](../lib/db/statement-timeout.ts) walks the error `cause` chain (code `57014` first, message fallback, cycle-guarded); wired into both `computeStageEligibilityPreview` and `segmentRulesPreview` ([lib/segment-rules-eval.ts](../lib/segment-rules-eval.ts)), which shared the same latent bug. Now a timeout degrades to `truncated` (block shows the count + "too large to compute the dedup preview — it still applies at send time") instead of 500'ing. Verified: unit test `scripts/test-statement-timeout.ts` (drizzle-wrapped/direct/message/nested/cyclic/negative shapes) + a forced 1ms-timeout run of the real function returning `truncated` without throwing. No schema change.

## 2026-07-07 — Campaign stages list no longer stalls 30–60s on behavioral lanes — docs: 04-features/behavioral-lanes, 07-conventions
- Opening a campaign with a behavioral split took 30–60s to render Stages. Root cause: each lane's Audience count ran a live-tier scan (`links⋈clicks` + `stage_sends`, ~6.6s cold) inline in `GET /api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages`, once per lane (3 per split), fired in parallel and CPU-contending. Fix: (1) new `computeLaneAudienceCountsBatch()` ([lib/audience-snapshot.ts](../lib/audience-snapshot.ts)) computes ALL lanes of a campaign in ONE query — the tier map is a single `MATERIALIZED` CTE reused per lane, the parent "alive" set built once; (2) lane counts are deferred off the list — the stages route returns lanes with `audience_count = null` (client renders `computing…`) and the client fetches the new `GET /api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/lane-counts` after first paint. List now paints in ~0.5s; lane numbers fill in shortly after. Batched counts proven byte-identical to the old per-lane `countStageRecipients` path across 25 real lane campaigns by [scripts/verify-lane-batch.ts](../scripts/verify-lane-batch.ts). No schema change.

## 2026-07-07 — Stage delete gate now covers behavioral lanes; atomic self-guarding delete — docs: 04-features
- Final whole-branch review of stage hard-delete found the gate only checked the TARGET stage's own send/result tables, so a never-sent behavioral parent could pass the gate and CASCADE-delete its sent lanes' data. `deleteStage` ([lib/stages/delete-stage.ts](../lib/stages/delete-stage.ts)) now blocks (409 `stage_has_send_data`) when the target OR any `parent_stage_id`-lane has `sent_at` set or rows in `stage_sends`/`stage_results_imports`/`stage_manual_sales`/`keitaro_stage_results`; a parent whose lanes are all truly empty still deletes (undo-split path unaffected). Gate + delete are now one atomic self-guarding `DELETE ... WHERE NOT EXISTS (...)` inside the transaction (was gate-SELECT-then-DELETE, a TOCTOU window for a concurrent Prepare). UI Delete action also now hides on a materialized/Prepared stage. No schema change.

## 2026-07-07 — Stage hard-delete + re-split unblock — docs: 04-features, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- New `DELETE /api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/[stageId]` (`stages.delete`, manager+): removes a stage that has no send/result data (`sent_at` null AND no `stage_sends`/`stage_results_imports`/`stage_manual_sales`/`keitaro_stage_results`), cleaned up by existing FK cascades. Sent/result-bearing stages stay archive-only. Deleting the extra A/B variants reverts the lone survivor to a normal stage.
- Re-split guards now ignore ARCHIVED siblings/lanes: A/B `/split` blocks only on live partners (`lib/stages/split-membership.ts`); behavioral `performBehavioralSplit` excludes archived lanes. Fixes stages stuck as "already split" after their variants were archived (e.g. `8_62_070126_1` "Day 4"). No schema change.

## 2026-07-07 — New segment rule `in_use_in_offer` ("In use in a specific offer") — migration 0092 — docs: 03-data-model, 04-features/audience-segments, CHANGELOG
- New segment audience rule type `in_use_in_offer` (value shape `offer_id`, operators `is`/`is_not`). Matches contacts already snapshotted into a `campaign_audience_pool` for a campaign with the chosen `offer_id`, where the campaign is `active`/`paused`/`completed` and still has ≥1 live stage (`draft`/`pending`/`sent`/`success`) — same live-campaign definition as `in_use_in_campaign_last_period`, scoped by offer instead of a time window. Migration `0092` widens `segment_rules_rule_type_check`. Eval case in `lib/segment-rules-eval.ts`; no client changes (offer picker is value-shape-driven).

## 2026-07-06 — Opt-out backfill from TextHub inbox CSV export (200-cap scroll-off remediation) — docs: 06-integrations, CHANGELOG
- **Cause.** TextHub's `?inbox=true` returns only the 200 most-recent messages, no pagination. Under STOP-reply bursts the 15-min poller couldn't keep up, so ~30% of opt-outs (Jun 29–Jul 5; 46% on Jul 4) scrolled off before ingest and were never suppressed — a live compliance gap, and undercounted per-stage opt-out rates.
- **Backfill.** One-time throwaway script (`scripts/`, deleted after) parsed the full inbox CSV export (2026-05-27 → 07-06, 25,710 `YES` rows), deduped against `texthub_inbound_events` by `(phone, received_at)`, and imported the **8,420 missing** opt-outs with byte-for-byte poller semantics: org-wide `opt_outs` (`source=sms_inbound`, real Mountain→UTC timestamp), live-72h attribution (`latestSendForAttribution`) → `opt_out_attributions` + `inbound_opt_out_count`/`opt_out_count` bumps + stage total-cost recompute. Idempotent/resumable via synthetic `backfill:<phone>:<received>` `provider_message_id`. Result: 4,063 attributed across 95 stages, 4,357 org-wide-only. No schema/code change — data + reporting only.
- **Follow-up (same day): attribution-date fix.** The Reports page (`app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts`) buckets per-stage opt-outs by `opt_out_attributions.created_at`, which the poller (and this backfill) leaves at `now()`. That made all backfilled attributions cluster on the import day. Corrected `opt_out_attributions.created_at = opt_outs.created_at` (the real STOP time) for the 4,298 rows whose ET report-day differed — opt-outs now report on the day they actually happened.
- **Poller fix (`lib/sends/poll-opt-outs.ts`).** Root of the same class of bug: the live poller stamped both `opt_outs.created_at` and `opt_out_attributions.created_at` at **poll time** (`now()`), so a STOP replied just before an ET-midnight poll landed on the wrong report day. Both inserts now use `anchorIso` — the STOP's parsed `received_at` (Mountain→UTC), with the existing `now()` fallback when unparseable — so opt-out and attribution always share the true reply timestamp and the Reports page/opt-out list agree on the day. Verified via `scripts/verify-poll-opt-outs.ts`.

## 2026-07-03 — Prepare progress UI + drop duplicate dedup index (migration 0091) — docs: 04-features/sms-send-pipeline, CHANGELOG
- **Prepare progress.** Materializing a stage is inherently O(recipients) index-maintenance (~3 ms/recipient across `links` + `stage_sends`) — a 10k stage takes ~30s, unavoidable at scale (no unused indexes to drop; enumeration adds a fixed ~3.2s dominated by the offer-exposure/opt-out reads). The Prepare dialog no longer freezes: it polls a new read-only `GET …/send/materialize-progress` (`{ materialized, complete }`) while `approve-send` runs and shows a live "Materializing N/total…" bar. No change to the send path.
- **Duplicate index.** Migration `0091` drops `stage_sends_org_phone_sent_at_idx` (added in 0090) — byte-identical to `stage_sends_org_phone_sent_idx` (0075), which serves the 1-hour dedup lookup unchanged. Removes double write-cost on `sent` updates.

## 2026-07-03 — Telegram report: resilient send (retry + visible failure alert) — docs: 04-features/crons, CHANGELOG
- **Why.** Two consecutive hourly reports (6 PM & 7 PM Warsaw = 16:00/17:00 UTC) were silently lost, then 8 PM recovered. Diagnosis ruled out config/code/DB/credentials/scheduler (all healthy — the 5-min pollers kept firing, metrics compute in ~3.5 s, prod Telegram creds valid): a transient Telegram/network blip on the fire-once-per-hour send, which has **no retry and no natural recovery until the next tick**, drops the whole hour with zero visibility outside Vercel logs.
- **Change.** [`app/api/cron/telegram-report/route.ts`](../app/api/cron/telegram-report/route.ts) now sends via `sendHtmlWithRetry` — **2 attempts, 8 s timeout each** (up from the shared 4 s default), 1 s backoff. `sendTelegramHtml(text, timeoutMs)` gained a configurable timeout param ([`lib/alerts/telegram.ts`](../lib/alerts/telegram.ts)). On final failure the handler still returns 500 **and** fires a best-effort plain-text `notifyTelegram` alert so a dropped report is noticed. Verified: `test-telegram-report-decision`/`-format` pass, eslint clean, no new tsc errors.

## 2026-07-03 — Split-leak fix (hash bucketing) + global 1-hour send-dedup (migration 0090) — docs: 04-features/sms-send-pipeline, 03-data-model, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- **Split leak.** A split stage's bucket was `row_number() over (order by contact_id) % splitTotal`, computed AFTER the resumable "exclude already-materialized" filter. On every resume the row numbers were reassigned over the shrunken remaining set, so the modulo picked a different subset and **leaked the sibling stage's half** into this one. Incident: campaign `8_62_070326_1` materialized 7,500 instead of 5,000 per split half (5,000 contacts in BOTH stages; 4,926 people got two live SMS). Fixed by a **stable per-contact hash bucket** (`splitBucketMatch`, [`lib/sends/split-bucket.ts`](../lib/sends/split-bucket.ts)) — a contact's bucket depends only on its own id, so excluding materialized rows can't move it. Applied to all six sites that reproduced the split (send recipients, all-phones export, frozen/draft/batched audience counts, dedup will_send preview, reconciliation). Test: [`scripts/test-split-resume-leak.ts`](../scripts/test-split-resume-leak.ts).
- **1-hour send-dedup (safety net).** New HARD gate in the drain: a phone that already received a message within `SEND_DEDUP_WINDOW_MS` (1h, [`lib/sends/dedup-window.ts`](../lib/sends/dedup-window.ts)) — org-wide, across every campaign/stage — is marked **`skipped_duplicate`** (migration [`0090`](../db/migrations/0090_stage_sends_skipped_duplicate.sql); terminal, not sent, not opted-out, not auto-retried) instead of being delivered.
