Telegram alert + in-app warning (Activity summary card, status badge/filter, stage operational status). Test: [`scripts/test-send-dedup.ts`](../scripts/test-send-dedup.ts).

## 2026-07-03 — Windowed, resumable send materialization (migration 0089) — docs: 04-features/sms-send-pipeline, 04-features/daily-volume-ui, 03-data-model, CHANGELOG
- **What.** "Prepare" (kickoff) materialized a stage's whole audience in ONE atomic transaction. Measured ~3.5 ms/recipient (enumerate + mint links + insert stage_sends across heavily-indexed tables), so it hit the 60s function limit around ~17K recipients and **rolled the whole batch back** — nothing prepared. Now materialization is **windowed + resumable**.
- **Core.** New `campaign_stages.materialized_at` (migration [`0089`](../../db/migrations/0089_stage_materialized_at.sql)) — the completeness signal, set only when the last window lands (backfilled for pre-existing stages that already had rows). `kickoffStageSend` commits in 2000-row windows under a time budget, enumerating only not-yet-materialized recipients, idempotent via `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` + the enumerate exclusion. A timeout/crash resumes from committed rows; a re-Prepare is a no-op (`already_pending` retired).
- **Never sends partial.** Scheduler Phase A resumes stages with `materialized_at IS NULL`; Phase B drains only `materialized_at IS NOT NULL`. So a half-built audience can't be sent — the cron finishes materialization, then drains.
- **Guardrail (shipped separately).** Kickoff/approve-send `maxDuration` 60→300.
- **Visibility.** A stage mid-materialization reads a new Indigo **"Materializing"** operational status (not "Prepared") so the operator sees steady progress instead of a stalled spinner; flips to Blue "Prepared" when complete. `materialized_at` surfaced on the stages + sends/today endpoints.
- Verified: `scripts/test-resumable-materialization.ts` (13/13 — fresh, idempotent, resume-after-partial with no duplicates, no-early-send gate), `tsc`, production build. Migration applied to prod.

## 2026-07-02 — Fix: prior-offer dedup (LAYER 3) self-cannibalized single-offer drips — docs: 04-features/content-dedup, CHANGELOG
- **What.** The offer-exposure exclusion (`campaigns.exclude_prior_offer_contacts`, eligibility LAYER 3) excluded EVERY contact in `offer_exposures` for the offer, with no same-campaign carve-out — unlike LAYER 1 (creative), which excepts `campaign_id <> current`. In a multi-stage campaign on one offer, stage 1's send registers the whole audience in `offer_exposures` under that campaign, so every LATER stage of the SAME campaign filtered them all out.
- **Symptom.** Campaign 235 "Kinzeno - 14508 - 01/07/26 (Manifestation)" stage 667 "Day 2" materialized **1** recipient instead of **9,672** (100% of the suppression came from its own Day 1). The stages "Audience" column showed 9,672 because that count does not apply the eligibility overlay; only the materialized `stage_sends` reflected the collapse.
- **Fix.** [`lib/sends/eligibility.ts`](../lib/sends/eligibility.ts) LAYER 3 gained `AND (campaign_id IS NULL OR campaign_id <> :currentCampaignId)`, mirroring LAYER 1. `exclude_prior_offer_contacts` now means "received this offer in a *different* campaign." The always-on creative layer still blocks re-sending identical content within a campaign.
- **Data.** The one affected stage (667) was recalled (its single stale `pending` row → `rejected`, `send_approved=false`) so it won't send; the operator recreates it. No other campaigns were created after the feature shipped, so none else affected.

## 2026-07-02 — Speed: campaigns/stages screens + send materialization — docs: 04-features/sms-send-pipeline, CHANGELOG
- **What.** Targeted at three user-reported slow spots. Measured first: the campaigns/stages DB queries are all **sub-millisecond** at current scale, so the felt slowness was round-trips + eager heavy fetches, not query time.
- **Stages screen.** `ClickReportSection` (heavy clicks aggregation) and `CampaignActivitySection` now mount via a new `<DeferUntilVisible>` (IntersectionObserver) wrapper, so they self-fetch only when scrolled near — off the initial-paint critical path. The `/activity` endpoint's 6 sequential queries now run in one `Promise.all` (ownership check stays first for the 404) — ~6 round-trips → ~2. The 2 per-stage correlated Keitaro subqueries (2×N subplans on a bare `stage_id`) were replaced by one grouped query keyed on `campaign_id` (indexed), mapped in like `sendCountsByStage` — same values.
- **Campaigns list.** The per-row `stage_count_total` correlated subquery was replaced by one grouped count batched alongside the existing `stageMeta` query.
- **Send materialization.** Insert chunk sizes bumped — `MINT_BATCH_CHUNK` 500→2000, `STAGE_SENDS_CHUNK` 500→1000 — cutting mint/insert round-trips 2–4× (each chunk is one round-trip). See [04-features/sms-send-pipeline.md](04-features/sms-send-pipeline.md).
- **Evaluated and NOT changed (measurement said no):** the opt-out `NOT EXISTS` → hash-join rewrite (result-equivalent but slightly slower at current pool sizes — `campaign_audience_pool` is a real table with stats, unlike the snapshot's set-op source); `campaign_stages(creative_id)` index (already exists — LAYER-2 dedup runs in 0.08 ms); mint/insert interleaving + resumable materialization (adds round-trips now / trades away materialization atomicity — deferred until single stages approach ~100K).

## 2026-07-02 — Telegram report: add Net Profit line — docs: 04-features/crons, CHANGELOG
- Added a **Net Profit** line (`revenue − spend`) after ROI in both the daily and hourly Telegram messages, sign-aware (`-$150.00` on a loss). Verified via `scripts/test-telegram-report-format.ts` (incl. a net-loss case); `eslint` clean.

## 2026-07-02 — Telegram daily + hourly performance report — docs: 04-features/crons, 06-integrations, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- **What.** New cron `/api/cron/telegram-report` ([route](../app/api/cron/telegram-report/route.ts)) sends performance reports to Telegram. **One hourly UTC trigger** (`vercel.json` `0 * * * *`); the handler decides per **current Warsaw time** (`Intl`/`date-fns-tz`, never offset math): Warsaw 11:00 → **daily** report for the previous ET day; Warsaw 16–23 (not Sun) and 00–01 (not Mon) → **hourly** today-so-far update; else `{ skipped:true }`. Sunday's window (Sun 16:00 → Mon 01:00 Warsaw) is excluded.
- **Metrics.** Sales, revenue, spend, opt-outs, ROI% for one ET day, **aggregated across all orgs** (no session in cron). Reuses `salesRevenueTotals()` (conversion-dated, dedup) so numbers reconcile with `/reports`; new `computeReportMetrics()` in [lib/reporting/report-snapshot.ts](../lib/reporting/report-snapshot.ts). "Delivered" = provider-accepted sends (`stage_sends.status='sent'`) — CamMan polls no DLR (§12). ROI `n/a` when spend=0; ratio `n/a` when delivered=0.
- **Telegram.** New non-swallowing `sendTelegramHtml()` (+ `escapeHtml`) in [lib/alerts/telegram.ts](../lib/alerts/telegram.ts): `parse_mode:"HTML"`, **throws → handler 500** on any failure so scheduler monitoring catches a broken report (vs. best-effort `notifyTelegram`). Auth: `CRON_SECRET` Bearer or `x-cron-secret` (401 otherwise); `?test=1` forces a send (still secret-protected). Fail-fast 500 if Telegram env missing.
- **Verified.** Live `?test=1` on dev → 200, real numbers (2026-07-01: sales 12 / rev $900 / spend $392.26 / 853 opt-outs / 38,502 delivered / ROI +129.4%) cross-checked against Supabase directly. Decision logic 17/17 edge cases (`scripts/test-telegram-report-decision.ts`); rendered messages incl. both `n/a` branches on real 2026-07-02 data — spend $0 & 0 delivered (`scripts/test-telegram-report-format.ts`); 401 no/bad secret; 500 on missing Telegram env; `tsc` + `eslint` clean.

## 2026-07-02 — Speed Tier 3: capped contacts-list count (kills the ~670ms every-load count) — docs: 07-conventions, 04-features/contacts-and-groups, CHANGELOG
- **What.** `/api/contacts/list` ran an exact `count(*)` on every load; at 752K rows that's ~670 ms (inherently O(rows) — an index can't help, proven by forcing the index path). Now the count is **capped**: it counts at most `COUNT_CAP+1` (10,000) rows via a `LIMIT` subquery. Under the cap it's the exact total; over it, the response sets `countApprox:true` + `totalCount:10000` and the UI shows "10,000+". **670 ms → 84 ms.**
- **Navigation stays correct.** The count no longer drives paging: the page query fetches `pageSize+1` and returns `hasMore`, so Previous/Next works past the cap. `DataTable` gained optional `hasMore` + `totalCountApprox` props (backward-compatible — other lists pass nothing and keep exact-count/`of Y` behavior). When approximate, the footer shows "N+" and "Page X" (no "of Y").
- **Exact counts preserved when it matters.** Any narrowing filter (search — already trigram-fast per Tier 1, segment, group, or the opt_outs/opt_ins/clickers views) returns a set under the cap, so the count stays exact. Only the unfiltered full-org browse shows "10,000+".
- Verified: capped-count speed + semantics (`scripts/verify-capped-count.ts`), Drizzle subquery SQL runs, `tsc`, production build. Scoped to the contacts endpoint (the measured pain); the `DataTable` mechanism is reusable for other large lists later.

## 2026-07-01 — Speed Tier 2: batched Keitaro pollers, staggered crons, parallelized queries, lazy campaign-detail dialogs — docs: 04-features/crons, CHANGELOG
- **What.** Second measured performance batch. Baseline via new `scripts/perf-baseline-tier2.ts`. No behavior change.
- **Keitaro pollers batched.** `poll-conversions.ts` and `poll-offer-reaches.ts` collected one round-trip per matched conversion (`UPDATE … WHERE id = …` in a loop). Now they fold matches in memory and flush a single `UPDATE … FROM (VALUES …)` per 500-row chunk. Measured round-trip overhead: **~12.4 s → ~53 ms** for 200 matches. Equivalence (incl. the ET-wall-clock → UTC `converted_at` cast) verified in a rolled-back transaction via `scripts/verify-keitaro-batch-update.ts`.
- **Crons staggered** (`vercel.json`). The four `*/15` jobs moved to off-5 offsets (`3/6/9/12`) so they no longer pile onto `:00/:15/:30/:45` with the `*/5` jobs — peak concurrency ~6 → ~2, easing cold-start + pooler-connection pressure. See [04-features/crons.md](04-features/crons.md).
- **Parallelized independent queries.** The stages-list route ran its inbound-STOP count and stage-send status counts sequentially → now one `Promise.all`. The `/api/keitaro/reports` route ran its opt-out / sent / manual-sales aggregates sequentially → now one `Promise.all`. Saves ~1 and ~2 round-trips of latency respectively.
- **Lazy campaign-detail dialogs.** `ResultsImportForm` (~793 lines), `StageSendPanel` (~517), `ManualResultsForm`, `ImportHistoryDialog` — each mounted only behind a `{state ? … : null}` dialog guard — now load via `next/dynamic({ssr:false})`, so their code (13.4/15.2/5.7/4.3 KB + transitive deps) is off the campaign-detail page's initial bundle.
- **Dropped from the plan by measurement:** the two proposed indexes (`keitaro_stage_results(stage_id)`, `campaign_audience_pool(org_id, campaign_id)`) — EXPLAIN showed both queries already index-served (0.1 ms; the pool PK already leads with `campaign_id`), so no index was added.

## 2026-07-01 — Speed Tier 1: trigram phone search, sargable send-state, dashboard bundle/refetch (migration 0088) — docs: 03-data-model, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- **What.** First batch of measured performance work. Baseline & after captured with the new `scripts/perf-baseline.ts` (`EXPLAIN ANALYZE`, real prod data: 752K contacts / 400K stage_sends).
- **Phone search (migration [`0088`](../db/migrations/0088_phone_trgm_indexes.sql)).** `pg_trgm` GIN indexes on `phone_number` for `contacts`/`opt_outs`/`opt_ins`/`clickers` so `ILIKE '%digits%'` list search is index-served. Contacts search `COUNT(*)`: **~820 ms → ~3 ms** (Seq Scan → Bitmap Index Scan). Built `CONCURRENTLY` in prod (no write lock); declared in [`db/schema.ts`](../db/schema.ts), managed in SQL (not in the drizzle snapshot). See [03-data-model.md](03-data-model.md).
- **Send-state `sent_today` (`lib/sends/send-state.ts`).** Replaced the non-sargable `(sent_at AT TIME ZONE 'ET')::date = today` (ran on every protected page, full index scan) with a half-open UTC range from the new `campaignDayBoundsUtc()` helper ([`lib/campaign-timezone.ts`](../lib/campaign-timezone.ts)): **~190 ms → ~20 ms**, same count (verified 33,489 = 33,489).
- **Dashboard.** Recharts moved to `next/dynamic({ssr:false})` ([`components/dashboard/chart-panel.tsx`](../components/dashboard/chart-panel.tsx)) so its ~340 KB chunk is off the landing page's initial bundle; refetch effect split so the 3 range-independent queries (active-campaigns/-stages, base-stats) fetch once on mount instead of re-firing on every date/compare toggle. `next.config.ts` gained `experimental.optimizePackageImports`.
- **CSV exports.** `maxDuration = 60` added to the segment-contacts and campaign-clickers export routes (were unbounded — risk of silent mid-stream truncation at scale).
- **Behavior:** none changed. All items are additive/equivalence-preserving; verified with `perf-baseline`, `verify-send-state-perf`, `verify-migration-integrity`, `tsc`, `eslint`.

## 2026-07-01 — Stage quick-schedule presets: 8 buttons + layout fix — docs: CHANGELOG
- **What.** The stage form's `ScheduledPresets` ([`components/campaigns/stage-form.tsx`](../components/campaigns/stage-form.tsx)) now offers Today/Tomorrow rows of chronological quick-picks (`9:30am · 10am · 6pm · 6:20pm`, ET) instead of just Today/Tomorrow 10am. Pills got a fixed height (`h-6`, `leading-none`) so they no longer reflow/jump on open. Label and Scheduled stay side-by-side on their row; the preset buttons render directly below the Scheduled input in the right column, and the auto-generated **Tracking ID** field now sits below Label in the left column (both cells wrapped, grid top-aligned via `items-start`) so they lay out on single lines instead of wrapping into a tall, cramped stack that pushed the stage layout. `CopyableId` ([`components/ui/copyable-id.tsx`](../components/ui/copyable-id.tsx)) gained an optional `inputClassName` prop; the stage Tracking ID passes `text-xs md:text-xs` so the full ID + copy button fit inside the narrower column (the shared `Input`'s `md:text-sm` otherwise wins at desktop widths). UI-only; no schema/API/behavior change to scheduling itself.
- **Attach-to-URL chips.** In `UrlParamChips` the UTM-tag chips now render **before** the `tracking_id` chip (was tracking_id-first). Combined with archiving the `subid5` and `subid2` UTM tags (registry data change — the stage form lists `status=active` only, so they no longer appear as chips), the row now reads `subid3` then `tracking_id`.

## 2026-06-30 — Content dedup Phase 2 — eligibility filtering goes live (migration 0087) — docs: 04-features/content-dedup, 04-features/sms-send-pipeline, 03-data-model, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- **What.** The exposure ledgers now actively SUPPRESS, not just record. New `campaigns.exclude_prior_offer_contacts` column (migration [`0087`](../db/migrations/0087_campaign_exclude_prior_offer.sql), boolean default false). Single shared eligibility builder [`lib/sends/eligibility.ts`](../lib/sends/eligibility.ts) (`buildStageEligibilityExclusions` / `applyEligibilityExcept` / `eligibilityUnion`) consumed by the send/export recipient query, reconciliation, and the preview — they can't diverge.
- **Send + export.** [`lib/sends/recipients.ts`](../lib/sends/recipients.ts) `stageRecipientsSql` gained an optional `eligibility` overlay (`base → EXCEPT layers → split`); threaded from `kickoff.ts` (send), the `export-phones` CSV route, and `preflight.ts`. LAYER 1 (saw this creative in a *different* campaign, `campaign_id IS NULL OR <> current` — the in-campaign-reuse exception) + LAYER 2 (in-flight send of this creative elsewhere) always apply when a creative is set (Edge A: null creative omits both); LAYER 3 (got this offer before) only when the campaign opts in. Opt-out suppression stays in the base audience (not re-added).
- **Reconciliation.** [`lib/sends/reconcile.ts`](../lib/sends/reconcile.ts) + the stage-send panel gained an `excluded_dedup` bucket: `pool = attempted + excluded(opt_out | filter | split | dedup) + gap`. Prevents a false materialization-gap alarm on deduped campaigns.
- **Toggle + counter + preview.** `exclude_prior_offer_contacts` surfaced as a toggle on both campaign forms (set in draft, locked after activation; read live at send time). Offers list + `GET /api/offers/[id]` return `distinct_contacts_used` (single-row `offer_exposure_counts` join) → a **"Leads used"** column. `computeStageEligibilityPreview` ([`lib/audience-snapshot.ts`](../lib/audience-snapshot.ts)) powers a debounced, timeout-aware breakdown in the stage form (*"N saw this creative · M got this offer · K will send"*); `will_send` subtracts the same layers then splits, so it equals the real materialized count.
- **Scope note.** The stages-list `audience_count` stays the pre-dedup addressable pool (perf-tuned batched query untouched); post-dedup will-send lives in the Prepare popup + preview. Recording manual CSV exports back into the ledger remains out of scope.
- **Verification.** [`scripts/verify-content-dedup-phase2.ts`](../scripts/verify-content-dedup-phase2.ts) — §7 acceptance tests all green on live data (cross-campaign suppression removed 10,000 / in-campaign reuse kept 10,000 / Edge A / offer toggle / preview==reality / pooler-safe). `npx tsc --noEmit` + eslint on changed files clean. Migrations 0086 + 0087 applied; integrity 88/88 green.

## 2026-06-30 — Content dedup & offer exposure — Phase 1 foundation (migration 0086) — docs: 03-data-model (+ERD), 04-features/content-dedup (new), 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- **What.** New ledgers backing three layers of content deduplication: `creative_exposures` (hard rule — same `creatives.id` never reaches the same `contacts.id` in a different campaign; `UNIQUE(org_id, contact_id, creative_id)`), `offer_exposures` (per-offer first-touch), and `offer_exposure_counts` (precomputed distinct-lead count per offer). Migration [`0086_content_dedup_exposure_ledger.sql`](../db/migrations/0086_content_dedup_exposure_ledger.sql): 3 tables + indexes + FKs + RLS (0085 precedent: org-scoped SELECT, no write policies) + two trigger functions.
- **Write-time maintenance.** `record_exposure_on_sent()` fires on `stage_sends` `AFTER INSERT WHEN status='sent'` and `AFTER UPDATE OF status` (→'sent'), resolving creative-via-stage + offer-via-campaign and inserting `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` (first sender wins). `bump_offer_exposure_count()` keeps `offer_exposure_counts` in sync (distinct by construction). Triggers (not app code) so no send path bypasses the ledger.
- **Backfill.** [`scripts/backfill-content-dedup-exposures.ts`](../scripts/backfill-content-dedup-exposures.ts) — idempotent, earliest `sent_at` wins the `campaign_id`. Run once after the migration applies.
- **Scope.** Foundation only — zero behavior change. The send-time eligibility anti-join (wired into the shared `stageRecipientsSql`, applies to send + CSV export), the per-campaign include/exclude toggle, the offer-page counter UI, and the async campaign-build preview are **Phase 2** (not yet built). "Used" = a `status='sent'` row; external-CSV sends are an accepted blind spot. `db/schema.ts` updated; `npx tsc --noEmit` clean. **Migration not yet applied to the live DB** (awaiting review checkpoint).

## 2026-06-30 — "Sent today" meter now counts the ET calendar day, not a rolling 24h — docs: 04-features/daily-volume-ui, CHANGELOG
- **Problem.** The `/sends/today` "Sent today (24h)" block ([`components/sends/volume-caps-meter.tsx`](../components/sends/volume-caps-meter.tsx)) was fed by `countSentSince(orgId, 86400)` — a rolling trailing-24h count. Opened mid-afternoon it folded in the prior day's evening sends, so it contradicted the same page's stage list (which filters by the ET calendar day) and didn't answer "what actually went out today".
- **Fix.** [`lib/sends/send-state.ts`](../lib/sends/send-state.ts) `getSendState()` now computes the displayed count as `count(stage_sends) WHERE (sent_at AT TIME ZONE 'America/New_York')::date = (now() AT TIME ZONE 'America/New_York')::date` (field renamed `today.sent_24h` → `today.sent_today`). Label changed to "Sent today (ET)". The breaker's own enforcement is untouched — the drain still uses rolling-24h `countSentSince` for the 24h ceiling; only the display value changed. Near midnight the meter can therefore read lower than the breaker's rolling view (noted in-component).

## 2026-06-29 — Keitaro sales dated by conversion day, not click day — docs: 04-features/keitaro-poll, 03-data-model (schema comments), CHANGELOG
- **Problem.** The Keitaro panel showed N conversions "today" but `/reports` showed N−1 (e.g. 10 vs 9 on 06-29). Root cause: the aggregate poll filled `keitaro_stage_results.stat_date` from Keitaro `report/build`'s `day` grouping, which attributes a conversion to the **originating click's day**, not the conversion day. So a sale that clicked Mon / converted Wed was filed under Mon — the campaign/click day. The reporting layer ([`lib/reporting/attribution.ts`](../lib/reporting/attribution.ts)) already documented `stat_date` as the **conversion** date, so the poll contradicted its own contract. Verified by probing both endpoints over a week: `report/build` per-day ≡ `conversions/log` bucketed by **click** time, exactly; the panel ≡ `conversions/log` by **conversion** time; both totals reconciled (76 = 76).
- **Fix.** [`lib/keitaro/poll.ts`](../lib/keitaro/poll.ts) now reads **two** endpoints over the same window: `report/build` for clicks/visits/redirects/cost (click-day, unchanged) and **`conversions/log`** for sales/checkouts/revenue, bucketed by each conversion's own `datetime` (conversion-day). New fold `applyConversionRowToAggregate`; `applyRowToAggregate` no longer credits conversions. Added `sub_id_3` to [`KEITARO_CONVERSION_COLUMNS`](../lib/keitaro/client.ts) (stage join key). Both fetches must succeed before any write (a clicks-only upsert could zero a stored conversion → degrade instead). Counts/totals are unchanged — only the date a sale is filed under. Unit test [`scripts/test-keitaro-visit-conversions.ts`](../scripts/test-keitaro-visit-conversions.ts) updated (20 checks green); tsc + eslint clean.
- **Backfill.** Re-date stored history with [`scripts/run-keitaro-redate-backfill.ts`](../scripts/run-keitaro-redate-backfill.ts) (= `pollKeitaro(db, { windowDays: 45 })`). 45-day total sales/revenue unchanged (108 / $7545); today moved 9 → 10. **Must deploy the code first** — the live `*/5` cron runs the old click-day code and re-clobbers the rolling 3-day window each tick until deployed.

## 2026-06-26 — Creatives list search now matches slug — docs: 04-features/campaigns-stages-creatives, CHANGELOG
- **What.** The `/creatives` list free-text search ([`lib/creatives/list-filters.ts`](../lib/creatives/list-filters.ts)) now `ILIKE`-matches `slug` in addition to `text` and `creative_id`, so creatives can be searched/filtered by their auto-generated slug. Because the clause is shared by `/api/creatives/list` and `/api/creatives/ids`, "select all matching" stays consistent. Search-box placeholder updated to "Search by text, creative ID, or slug…".

## 2026-06-24 — Enable RLS on `stage_manual_sales` + `opt_out_attributions` (migration `0085`) — docs: 03-data-model, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- **Why.** Supabase advisor flagged `rls_disabled_in_public` on both tables. With RLS off, the anon key (shipped in the frontend bundle) can read/write every row via PostgREST — and `stage_manual_sales` directly drives reported revenue. Second RLS remediation after `geoip_cache` (`0066`).
- **Change.** Migration [0085](../db/migrations/0085_stage_manual_sales_optout_attributions_rls.sql) enables RLS and — because both are tenant tables with `org_id` (unlike infra `geoip_cache`, which is policy-less) — adds an `org_id = current_org_id()` **SELECT** policy to each. No write policies: both tables are written exclusively by the privileged server Drizzle connection (which bypasses RLS), mirroring the `stage_sends` (`0050`) precedent. Verified every read/write path (`app/api/keitaro/reports`, `lib/reporting/attribution.ts`, the manual-results route, `lib/sends/poll-opt-outs.ts`, backfill scripts) uses `@/db/client`, so app functionality is unbroken.
- **Apply.** Run `npm run db:migrate` against the target `DATABASE_URL`, then `npx tsx scripts/verify-migration-integrity.ts`; advisor should stop flagging both tables.

## 2026-06-24 — Document `keitaro_stage_results.cost` is always 0 (do-not-use guard) — docs: 03-data-model, CHANGELOG
- **Why.** `keitaro_stage_results.cost` is Keitaro ad-platform spend, which is always 0 here (we don't buy traffic through Keitaro). Any ROI/EPC/profit built off it would be wrong (e.g. infinite ROI from cost 0). Real stage cost is the SMS send spend in `campaign_stages.total_cost`.
- **Audit.** Confirmed no ROI or EPC anywhere derives cost from this column: the campaign-detail ROI tiles + per-stage ROI use `campaign_stages.total_cost`; the manual-results ROI estimate uses `total_cost`; the `/reports` route (`app/api/keitaro/reports`) folds this column in but **overwrites** it with `campaign_stages.total_cost` before computing profit; stored/derived `epc` everywhere is `revenue / clicks` (revenue-derived, not cost). The unused `/api/keitaro/results` endpoint exposes a Keitaro-cost-based `profit` but has no caller.
- **Change.** Added a `DO NOT USE` comment to the `cost` column (and a clarifying note to `epc`) in [db/schema.ts](../db/schema.ts) and flagged it in the data-model table. No schema/migration change, no behavior change.

## 2026-06-24 — Standardize all sales/revenue reporting on conversion date (`stat_date`) — docs: 07-conventions, 03-data-model, 04/keitaro-poll, CHANGELOG
- **Problem.** Sales/revenue were counted two ways that silently disagreed: the `/reports` page bucketed by Keitaro conversion day (`keitaro_stage_results.stat_date` — e.g. 06-23 = 10 sales / $741) while the dashboard stats + daily-activity charts bucketed by the stage's **send day** (06-23 = 8 sales / $600). Conversions lag sends, so the two windows differ, and nothing defined which to use.
- **Fix.** New single source of truth [lib/reporting/attribution.ts](../lib/reporting/attribution.ts) with `ATTRIBUTION_BASIS = 'stat_date'` and shared helpers (`salesRevenueTotals`, `salesRevenueByDay`, `manualSalesByStageInRange`). Revenue + Keitaro sales date by `stat_date`; manual sales date by the `stage_manual_sales` ledger **entry date**; the two sales sources combine per stage via `combineSales` (max). Rewired [app/api/dashboard/stats/route.ts](../app/api/dashboard/stats/route.ts), [app/api/dashboard/daily-activity/route.ts](../app/api/dashboard/daily-activity/route.ts), and [app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts](../app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts) (manual baseline moved from `sent_at` to the ledger). Dashboard now shows 06-23 = 10 sales / $741, matching `/reports`. Send-day metrics (SMS, cost, opt-outs, clickers) stay send-dated; a code note marks that daily ROI mixes windows (revenue conversion-dated, spend send-dated — washes out over a steady period).
- **Migration 0084** (`0084_redate_manual_sales_backfill.sql`): re-dates the migration-0079 backfill ledger rows (`entered_by IS NULL`, all stamped at one `now()`) to each stage's effective send day, so historical manual sales spread realistically across the calendar instead of lumping on a single date. Data-only, idempotent. **Must be applied via `npm run db:migrate` before/at deploy** — until then manual sales read as lumped on the backfill date.
- **Optional dual view** ("booked today" stat_date vs "maturing" send-day cohort side-by-side) flagged but NOT built — deferred as non-trivial.

## 2026-06-24 — Fix: Keitaro poll credits conversions stranded on the visit campaign — docs: 04/keitaro-poll, CHANGELOG
- **Bug.** A URL mistake (broken landing→offer redirect) left conversions attached to the `gk-lp-visits` campaign instead of the offer campaign. The poll's fold credited conversions/revenue **only** from offer-campaign rows and dropped them on visit rows, so those sales never appeared in CamMan (5 conversions / $375 across stages 587/588/590 on ET 2026-06-24 read as $0). Manual `sales_count` couldn't fix revenue (sourced solely from `keitaro_stage_results.revenue`, which the poll rewrites every 5 min).
- **Fix.** [lib/keitaro/poll.ts](../lib/keitaro/poll.ts) extracts the per-row fold into an exported `applyRowToAggregate` and credits **checkouts/sales/revenue from every report row** regardless of campaign; only **clicks** are still split visit vs. redirect (and `cost` rides the offer side). Safe against double-counting — each Keitaro conversion has exactly one `campaign_id`, so it appears in exactly one row (verified: 0 cross-campaign conversion splits over 30 days). No-op for healthy campaigns (visit rows normally carry no conversions). No schema change.
- **Test.** [scripts/test-keitaro-visit-conversions.ts](../scripts/test-keitaro-visit-conversions.ts) — pure unit test of the fold (18 assertions): visit-row conversions credited, clicks split correctly, combined fold sums without double-count, healthy visit row unchanged.
- **Backfill.** Re-running `pollKeitaro(db, { windowDays: 7 })` resynced the three stages (587→2 sales/$150, 588→2 sales+2 checkouts/$150, 590→1 sale+1 checkout/$75). Durable once deployed — the live cron must run the fixed code or it re-clobbers these rows on its next tick.

## 2026-06-24 — Fix: inbound opt-out credited to the LATEST stage only (no fan-out) — docs: 04/crons, 04/keitaro-poll, 03-data-model, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- **Bug.** The opt-out poller credited **every** stage that sent to a number within the 72h window — one `opt_out_attributions` row per stage. A sequence campaign that messaged the same lead 2–3× counted that lead's single STOP 2–3×. On ET 2026-06-23 this turned 530 real opt-outs into 996 attribution rows, so `/reports` "Avg Opt-out" read ~3.07% vs the true ~1.6%.
- **Fix.** [lib/sends/poll-opt-outs.ts](../lib/sends/poll-opt-outs.ts) now credits the **single most-recent send across all stages** in the window via a new `latestSendForAttribution` helper (`ORDER BY sent_at DESC, stage_id DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1` — deterministic tie-break: higher `stage_id`, then higher `stage_send_id`). Exactly **one** `opt_out_attributions` row + one stage's `inbound_opt_out_count`/`opt_out_count`/`total_cost` bump per STOP. Unattributed + idempotency (`texthub_inbound_events` claim + `ON CONFLICT`) unchanged; `attributed` summary is now 0 or 1 per STOP. No schema change — the existing partial index `stage_sends_org_phone_sent_idx` already serves the global-latest lookup.
- **CSV imports unaffected:** the result-import path ([app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/[stageId]/import/route.ts](<../app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/[stageId]/import/route.ts>)) never writes `opt_out_attributions` — it bumps `opt_out_count` for the one stage being imported — so it already attributes to a single stage. Left as-is.
- **Backfill.** [scripts/backfill-optout-latest-stage.ts](../scripts/backfill-optout-latest-stage.ts) collapses historical fan-out (keep latest-stage row per opt_out, re-derive each affected stage's counters + `total_cost`). Dry-run by default, `--apply` to commit; idempotent. Dry-run on the shared DB: 4191 → 3014 attribution rows (= distinct opt_outs), 1177 collapsed, 21 stages. **Not run with `--apply` yet.**
- **Tests.** [scripts/test-optout-latest-stage.ts](../scripts/test-optout-latest-stage.ts) (rolled-back, real schema): 3-stages→latest, tie-break, unattributed, idempotent re-credit — all green. Existing `verify-poll-opt-outs.ts` harness untouched + green.

## 2026-06-24 — Fix: revenue is sourced from `keitaro_stage_results.revenue`, never `sales × CPA` + effective-dated CPA history — docs: 03-data-model, 07-conventions, 04/keitaro-poll, 04/registry, CHANGELOG
- **Bug.** `offers.payout_cpa` is a single mutable value overwritten when a network changes the CPA mid-flight (Kinzeno 14508 went $60→$66→$75 on 2026-06-23). Any revenue computed as `sales × payout_cpa` (via the `sales_payout_each` snapshot) was wrong for every prior period. `keitaro_stage_results.revenue` already stores the correct real per-conversion payout at sync time. Verified: Kinzeno 14418 = **$621** (Keitaro) vs $675 (CPA-recompute); Kinzeno 14508 = $1,497 vs $1,320 (figures grew from the task's $1,422/$1,260 as live polling added a conversion).
- **(A) Source of truth.** Replaced every `sales × CPA` revenue figure with a SUM over `keitaro_stage_results.revenue`: [dashboard/stats](../app/api/dashboard/stats/route.ts), [dashboard/daily-activity](../app/api/dashboard/daily-activity/route.ts), the campaign detail per-stage column + rollup ([campaigns/[id]/page.tsx](<../app/(protected)/campaigns/[id]/page.tsx>), fed by a new `keitaro_revenue` field on [stages/list](../app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/route.ts)), and the creatives EPC ([creatives/list](../app/api/creatives/list/route.ts)). `/reports` already summed it. The manual-results form keeps a clearly-labeled sales×CPA *estimate* (never persisted). `offers.payout_cpa` commented as current-rate cache only in [db/schema.ts](../db/schema.ts).
- **(B) Frozen per-conversion rate.** Migration `0082` adds `keitaro_stage_results.payout_at_conversion` (= `revenue / NULLIF(sales,0)`), backfilled per row and written by the Keitaro poll ([lib/keitaro/poll.ts](../lib/keitaro/poll.ts)) at sync time, so a CPA edit can never retro-change a recorded rate.
- **(C) CPA history.** Migration `0083` adds `offer_payouts` (effective-dated rows, partial unique index → one open row per offer, RLS org-scoped). Backfilled one current row per CPA offer. The offers create/update path ([offers POST](../app/api/offers/route.ts) / [PATCH](../app/api/offers/[id]/route.ts)) now closes the current row + opens a new one on a CPA change instead of overwriting; `offers.payout_cpa` stays updated as the cache.
- Migrations applied to the shared DB; `verify-migration-integrity` green (84/84). Verified backfills (28/28 keitaro rows stamped, 13/13 offers seeded with one open row) and the close/open write path (rolled-back tx: @60 closed, @88 opened).

## 2026-06-24 — Fix: Reports Cost column sources real SMS spend, not Keitaro ad-spend — docs: 04/keitaro-poll, CHANGELOG
- **Bug.** The `/reports` Cost column (and the Cost/Profit totals) read `keitaro_stage_results.cost` — Keitaro's ad-platform spend metric, which is always 0 for these SMS campaigns — so every cell showed 0.
- **Fix.** [app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts](../app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts) now selects `campaign_stages.total_cost` (the auto-calculated `cost_per_sms × (sends + opt_outs)`, owned by [lib/stages/total-cost.ts](../lib/stages/total-cost.ts)) and uses it as the funnel's `cost`. Like total_sent / manual sales it has no per-day timeline, so it's attributed to the stage's `sent_at` and counted only when that lands in the selected range (0 otherwise). Campaign rollups sum it across stages; grand total replaces the per-row Keitaro sum. `Profit = Revenue − Cost` follows automatically. No UI or schema change.

## 2026-06-23 — Fix: copied/split stages no longer inherit a stale send date or wrong-stage `sub_id3` — docs: 04/sms-send-pipeline, 04/behavioral-lanes, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- **Bug 1 — stale send date auto-fires.** Every stage-copy path inherited the parent's `scheduled_at`; a past date made the send pipeline fire the new stage immediately on approval. All four copy paths now set `scheduled_at = null` ([manual duplicate](../app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/[stageId]/duplicate/route.ts), [behavioral-split](../lib/stages/behavioral-split.ts), [A/B split](../app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/[stageId]/split/route.ts), [campaign duplicate](../app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/duplicate/route.ts)). Added a hard `no_schedule` guard in `kickoffStageSend` ([lib/sends/kickoff.ts](../lib/sends/kickoff.ts)) — a NULL `scheduled_at` is **never** sent and **never** treated as "now"; the explicit Send now action stamps `scheduled_at = now()` before kickoff ([approve-send route](../app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/[stageId]/send/approve-send/route.ts) + body `send_now`, wired in [stage-prepare-dialog.tsx](../components/campaigns/stage-prepare-dialog.tsx)).
- **Bug 2 — wrong-stage `sub_id3`.** The copied `full_url` kept the parent's `sub_id3`, misattributing clicks. New surgical helper `setUrlParam` ([lib/stage-url.ts](../lib/stage-url.ts)) rewrites only `sub_id3` to the new stage's tracking id, preserving `sub_id1` (L2 attribution) and all other params; applied in every copy path after the new tracking id is assigned. (Tracked-mode minted links already self-corrected via the kickoff rebuild — verified against live `link_destinations` — so no short-link backfill is needed; the stale value was operator-facing/manual-mode only.)
- Existing guards (SEND_ENABLED, org/provider pause, quiet hours, claim lock) untouched. Tests: `scripts/test-set-url-param.ts` (8/8), `scripts/test-stage-copy-invariants.ts` (10/10); `scripts/test-kickoff-fullurl.ts` fixture given a `scheduled_at`. No schema change.

## 2026-06-22 — Stage Total Cost only calculates after the send — docs: 03-data-model, 04/campaigns-stages-creatives, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- **Rule.** The auto Total Cost now stays `$0` until the stage has been sent — it no longer computes at creation time. "Sent" = `sent_at IS NOT NULL` (an API fire or a "Mark as sent" click) **OR** `sms_count > 0` (hand-entered results imply the send happened). The gate lives in the shared SQL recompute ([lib/stages/total-cost.ts](../lib/stages/total-cost.ts)) and is mirrored in the manual-results form preview ([components/campaigns/manual-results-form.tsx](../components/campaigns/manual-results-form.tsx), new `isSent` prop) and the backfill ([scripts/backfill-stage-total-cost.ts](../scripts/backfill-stage-total-cost.ts), re-run: 6 stages corrected, 0 mismatches). No schema change.

## 2026-06-22 — Fix: stage Total Cost counts real sends, not just sms_count — docs: 03-data-model, 04/campaigns-stages-creatives, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- **Bug.** The auto Total Cost used `cost_per_sms × (sms_count + opt_out_count)`, but API/tracked stages leave `sms_count = 0` (the dispatched messages live in `stage_sends`), so a stage that sent 9.7k messages costed on its 84 opt-outs alone (`$0.84` instead of `$97.76`). Now `sends = GREATEST(sms_count, accepted stage_sends)` — the count of provider-accepted rows (`status='sent'`) for tracked stages, `sms_count` for manual/CSV stages ([lib/stages/total-cost.ts](../lib/stages/total-cost.ts)). The manual-results route now routes its auto path through the same SQL recompute (single source of truth); the form's live preview takes a `sentCount` so it matches. Re-ran [scripts/backfill-stage-total-cost.ts](../scripts/backfill-stage-total-cost.ts) (42 stages corrected, 0 mismatches remaining).

## 2026-06-22 — Stage Total Cost auto-derives from cost-per-SMS (migration 0081) — docs: 03-data-model, 04/campaigns-stages-creatives, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- **Feature — auto Total Cost.** A stage's `total_cost` now defaults to `cost_per_sms × (sms_count + opt_out_count)`, sourced from the assigned provider phone ([lib/stages/total-cost.ts](../lib/stages/total-cost.ts)). Opt-out replies count toward the multiplier (STOPs are billed like sends). Recomputed wherever the inputs move: the manual-results save ([route](../app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/[stageId]/manual-results/route.ts)), the opt-out poller after a STOP bumps `opt_out_count` ([lib/sends/poll-opt-outs.ts](../lib/sends/poll-opt-outs.ts)), and the stage PATCH on a `provider_phone_id` change ([route](../app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/[stageId]/route.ts)).
- **Override flag — `total_cost_manual` (migration 0081).** New `campaign_stages.total_cost_manual boolean DEFAULT false`. The manual-results form gains an **Auto-calculate total cost** switch with a live `$rate × (sent + opt-outs)` readout; turning it off lets the operator type an explicit figure (`total_cost_manual=true`). A CSV import carrying a real provider cost also takes the field manual — replacing a previously auto-derived value on the first cost-bearing import, accumulating thereafter ([import route](../app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/[stageId]/import/route.ts)). Migration backfills existing non-zero costs to `manual=true` so live data isn't clobbered. List/detail GETs expose `cost_per_sms` + `total_cost_manual`. **Requires `npm run db:migrate` before deploy.**

## 2026-06-22 — Reports: add CR, % column (clickers ÷ total sent) — docs: 04/keitaro-poll, CHANGELOG
- **Feature — click-through rate column.** The `/reports` table gains a sortable **CR, %** column next to Clickers: `clickers / total_sent`, rendered as a percent. Computed server-side in [app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts](../app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts) as a `click_rate` fraction (per-stage, per-campaign rollup, and grand totals), sharing the renamed `rateOfSent` helper with `opt_out_rate`. Zero when nothing was sent (no divide-by-zero). UI in [app/(protected)/reports/page.tsx](../app/(protected)/reports/page.tsx). No schema change.

## 2026-06-22 — Emergency send hard-stop on Today's sends + reject past-scheduled stages — docs: 03-data-model, 04/sms-send-pipeline, 04/daily-volume-ui, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- **Feature — emergency hard-stop (migration 0080).** New `org_settings.sends_paused` flag, a dedicated org-wide kill-switch independent of the daily `sends_enabled` master switch. A "Hard stop" button on the **Today's sends** screen ([page](../app/(protected)/sends/today/page.tsx), manager+/`campaigns.drain`) flips it via the new `POST /api/sends/pause`; while engaged a red banner offers "Proceed (resume sending)". The drain ([lib/sends/drain.ts](../lib/sends/drain.ts)) re-reads it fresh each batch — entry refusal `send_paused_org`, mid-run `stopReason = "org_paused"` — so engaging it halts any in-flight send at the next batch boundary and refuses new ones until cleared. Helper `getOrgSendsPaused` ([lib/sends/org-send-flag.ts](../lib/sends/org-send-flag.ts)); state surfaced on `GET /api/sends/state`; flips audited in `org_setting_events` (`setting_key='sends_paused'`). **Requires `npm run db:migrate` before deploy.**
- **Rule — a stage can't be scheduled in the past.** Stage `POST`/`PATCH` ([routes](../app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/)) reject a `scheduled_at` earlier than now (60s grace), via the shared pure guard [lib/sends/schedule-guard.ts](../lib/sends/schedule-guard.ts) (`isScheduledAtInPast`), mirrored client-side in the stage form (inline error + blocked save). PATCH only enforces it when the value actually changes, so unrelated edits to a historically-scheduled stage still save.

## 2026-06-21 — Reports: Sales = max(manual, Keitaro) not sum (fix double-count) + Avg Opt-out badge — docs: 04/keitaro-poll, CHANGELOG
- **Bug — Sales double-counted a sale present in both Keitaro and the manual tally.** Sales was **additive** (`manual sales_count + Keitaro conversions`). When the same real sale was both tracked by Keitaro and tallied manually, it counted twice — e.g. stage `8_62_061226_3_s4_c124` showed **2** for one real sale (Keitaro 1 + manual 1). Sales is now **`max(manual, Keitaro)`** per stage via the shared `combineSales()` helper ([lib/stage-results.ts](../lib/stage-results.ts)): it dedupes the overlap while preserving whichever source saw more — Keitaro when it's ahead, the manual baseline on stages where Keitaro under-counts (incomplete `sub_id` capture). Applied to both `/reports` ([app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts](../app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts), `max(0, manual − Keitaro)` top-up in range) and the campaign-detail page's 3 sales sites ([app/(protected)/campaigns/[id]/page.tsx](../app/(protected)/campaigns/[id]/page.tsx)). No data migration; read-time math only. The dashboard (manual `sales_count` only, never additive) is unaffected.
- **Feature — Avg Opt-out stat card on `/reports`.** New grand-total badge showing the period's aggregate opt-out rate (grand opt-outs ÷ grand total sent), alongside the existing Clickers/Offer Redirect/Sales/Revenue/Cost/Profit cards ([app/(protected)/reports/page.tsx](../app/(protected)/reports/page.tsx)). Uses the already-computed `totals.opt_out_rate` — no API change.

## 2026-06-20 — Reports: fix Total Sent for manual sends + anchor manual sales to the send date — docs: 04/keitaro-poll, CHANGELOG
- **Bug 1 — Total Sent showed 0 for manually-sent stages.** `/api/keitaro/reports` counted only `stage_sends` (the TextHub API pipeline). Almost all sends happen manually in external provider tools, which have no `stage_sends` rows, so every manual stage read 0. Total Sent now uses the stage's `sms_count` for `link_mode='manual'` campaigns (gated by `sent_at` in range) and keeps the per-recipient `stage_sends` count for `link_mode='tracked'`. The two are mutually exclusive → no double-counting ([app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts](../app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts)).
- **Bug 2 — Sales ignored the date filter (showed lifetime).** The manual-sales side summed `stage_manual_sales` deltas by ledger entry time, but the migration-0079 backfill stamped every pre-existing total at `now()`, so any window covering the backfill date showed the full lifetime manual sales. Manual sales are now attributed to the stage's single send moment: the stage's `sales_count` is added to the in-range Keitaro conversions only when `sent_at` lands in the window. The `stage_manual_sales` ledger remains for audit/current-total but no longer drives the report. No data migration needed.
- Both fixes follow the **activity-date scoping** model: a stage's manual counters have no per-event timeline, so they ride the send activity's date (`sent_at`). Requires the stage to have been marked `sent` (which stamps `sent_at`).

## 2026-06-19 — Spam check: stop failing on new creatives (cold-start hardening) — docs: 04/spam-classifier, CHANGELOG
- **Bug:** scoring a *new* creative (cache miss → real classifier call) often failed because the Cloud Run classifier scales to zero and the first request's cold start blew the 10s timeout. The fallback (score 50) was then **cached permanently**, pinning the text to a bogus "50 / NOT SPAM" even after the service recovered.
- Per-attempt timeout 10s → **25s** (`CLASSIFIER_TIMEOUT_MS`); provider **retries once** on timeout/network errors only (not on HTTP non-2xx); `/api/spam/score` gains `maxDuration = 60` ([lib/spam/providers/classifier.ts](../lib/spam/providers/classifier.ts), [app/api/spam/score/route.ts](../app/api/spam/score/route.ts)).
- **Failures are no longer cached** ([lib/spam/score.ts](../lib/spam/score.ts)) — a transient failure no longer poisons the cache; the next check re-scores.
- Strip shows **"Couldn't score — try Re-check"** instead of a fake verdict on failure ([components/spam/spam-check-strip.tsx](../components/spam/spam-check-strip.tsx)).
- Recommended permanent fix (ops, outside repo): run the classifier always-on (self-hosted / `min-instances=1`) — one-env-var change app-side (`CLASSIFIER_URL`).

## 2026-06-20 — Reports: every metric scoped to the selected date range + dated manual-sales ledger — migration 0079 — docs: 03-data-model, 04/keitaro-poll, CHANGELOG
- **Bug:** `/reports` mixed lifetime counters into a date-ranged view — Sales showed the operator's lifetime manual `sales_count` (e.g. a stage showed 5 sales in a 2-day window with no sales), and opt-outs read the lifetime `campaign_stages.inbound_opt_out_count`. Fixed so **all** metrics reflect only the selected ET period ([app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts](../app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts)):
  - **Sales** = Keitaro conversions in range (`keitaro_stage_results.sales`, bounded by `stat_date`) **+ manual sales entered in range**. Since `campaign_stages.sales_count` is an overwrite-on-save integer with no date, a new per-entry ledger **`stage_manual_sales`** (migration 0079) records the signed delta on each manual-results save, dated to the save; the report sums in-range deltas and adds them on top. `SUM(delta)` per stage == `sales_count`. Manual revenue is not added (report Revenue stays Keitaro-only).
  - **Opt-outs** = count of `opt_out_attributions` credited to the stage with `created_at` in range (was the lifetime stage counter).
  - **Total Sent** (new column) = `stage_sends` with `status='sent'` and `sent_at` in range (failed/rejected/pending/filtered excluded).
  - **OptOut, %** (new column) = `opt_outs / total_sent`, rendered as a %.
  - Range → UTC bounds via `fromZonedTime` (America/New_York); exclusive upper bound built off the next calendar day (not +24h) so it's DST-correct. Opt-outs / total-sent / manual-sales each computed by one grouped query over the stages in view; all summed into campaign rollups + grand totals, both new fields added to the sortable set + response `totals`.
- **Ledger writes:** the manual-results endpoint ([app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/[stageId]/manual-results/route.ts](../app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/[stageId]/manual-results/route.ts)) now records `delta = new − old sales_count` (when non-zero) into `stage_manual_sales` in the same transaction as the stage update, stamped with the actor. Migration 0079 backfills existing `sales_count > 0` totals as one delta dated `now()` ("treat as entered now").
- Page ([app/(protected)/reports/page.tsx](../app/(protected)/reports/page.tsx)) renders the **Total Sent** + **OptOut, %** columns. Schema: [db/schema.ts](../db/schema.ts) `stage_manual_sales`. Typecheck clean. **Migration 0079 must be applied (`npm run db:migrate`) before deploy.**

## 2026-06-19 — Sales = manual + Keitaro conversions (additive), at stage + report level — docs: 04/keitaro-poll, CHANGELOG
- Keitaro's bare `sales` metric is always 0 (network fires only `lead` postbacks); the payable events are Keitaro `conversions` (= leads+sales). Mapped Keitaro **`conversions` → Sales** ([lib/keitaro/poll.ts](../lib/keitaro/poll.ts), `keitaro_stage_results.sales` now stores conversions). But Keitaro tracking is incomplete (~11 conversions vs the operator's ~104 manual sales), so Sales is **ADDITIVE, not authoritative**: the poll no longer writes `campaign_stages.sales_count` (the manual baseline is preserved), and the Keitaro conversion count is added on top **at read time**, only for stages that have conversions.
- Stage Results column + campaign totals: Sales = `sales_count` (manual) + `keitaro_sales_count` (new correlated-subquery field on the stages API), Revenue/ROI rates the combined count. Reports (`/api/keitaro/reports`) seeds each stage's funnel tally with the manual `sales_count` so stage rows, the Stage/Campaign rollup, and grand totals all show manual+Keitaro; Keitaro revenue (real per-conversion) unchanged.
- Data: reset 3 stages whose `sales_count` a test-poll had overwritten back to manual baseline 0 (operator-confirmed they had no manual sales). Typecheck clean. NOTE: the deployed (pre-this-commit) poll propagated `keitaro_stage_results.sales` into `sales_count`; after this deploys, a wide re-poll + one-time reset of any conversion-having stage's `sales_count` to its manual baseline completes the cutover.

## 2026-06-19 — Reports: group by Stage or Campaign (sales per campaign) — docs: 04/keitaro-poll, CHANGELOG
- `/reports` already showed per-stage Sales; added a **Group by: Stage / Campaign** toggle so the funnel (incl. **sales**) can be read per campaign. API gains a `groupBy` param ([app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts](../app/api/keitaro/reports/route.ts)): campaign rollups fold every stage of a campaign into one row (clickers/redirect/sales/revenue/cost summed via new `mergeFunnel` in [lib/keitaro/funnel.ts](../lib/keitaro/funnel.ts), opt-outs summed across the campaign's stages — captured once per stage so no per-day double-count, rates re-derived), carrying `stage_count` instead of stage fields. Page shows a `Stages` count column in campaign mode. Verified rollup sums on live data; typecheck clean. (Sales still 0 — Keitaro reports none, see prior entry — but now visible at both granularities.)

## 2026-06-19 — Stage Results column shows Checkout + Sales; Keitaro 0-sales investigated — docs: 04/keitaro-poll, CHANGELOG
- Per-stage **Results** column on the campaigns detail page now renders `Clicks · Checkout · Sales · CTR · OptOut` (was `Clicks · CTR · OptOut`) — surfacing the already-Keitaro-fed `checkout_click_count` and `sales_count` ([app/(protected)/campaigns/[id]/page.tsx](../app/(protected)/campaigns/[id]/page.tsx)). No new data source; display only.
- Force-synced the Keitaro `*/5` aggregate poll (fetched 143, upserted 108, no errors) — data current.
- **Investigated why `sales_count` is 0 everywhere:** probed Keitaro directly with no status filter (Jun 1–19) → 11 conversions, ALL status `lead` ($668), zero `sale`/`deposit`/`approved`. Not a mapping bug — the network fires only `lead` postbacks, so payable conversions land in **Checkout Clicks**, and Sales stays 0 until the network sends `status=sale` (Keitaro/network-side config). Documented as an operational note in [keitaro-poll.md](04-features/keitaro-poll.md).

## 2026-06-19 — Backfill: sync `opt_out_count` display figure to inbound STOPs — docs: CHANGELOG
- The earlier attribution backfill corrected `inbound_opt_out_count` but left `opt_out_count` (the figure the per-stage Results column shows) stale on tz-bug-affected stages. The live opt-out poller mirrors `opt_out_count = inbound_opt_out_count`, so most stages self-healed on their next STOP; [`scripts/backfill-optout-attributions.ts`](../scripts/backfill-optout-attributions.ts) now also mirrors **upward only** (`WHERE inbound_opt_out_count > opt_out_count`) to catch stages that won't get another STOP — never reduces a stage whose `opt_out_count` is higher (CSV/manual results share the column). Ran on prod: 9 stages synced; stage `8_62_061826_1_s2_c122` Results opt-out 0 → 122. No drift remains.

## 2026-06-19 — Refine: TextHub `received_at` uses `America/Denver` (DST-aware), not a fixed −6 — docs: 04/crons, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- Operator confirmed TextHub's inbox clock is US Mountain Time. Mountain observes DST, and the −6 offset was measured in June (MDT); a hardcoded −6 would be 1h wrong every winter (MST/−7). Switched `parseProviderReceivedAt` from a fixed `TEXTHUB_RECEIVED_AT_UTC_OFFSET_HOURS = -6` to interpreting the wall-clock in `TEXTHUB_RECEIVED_AT_TIMEZONE = "America/Denver"` via `date-fns-tz` `fromZonedTime` — the IANA zone resolves the offset per-date automatically. No live behavior change right now (June = MDT = −6); only winter correctness. Test now asserts both a summer (−6) and winter (−7) case. Resolves the DST caveat from the prior entry.

## 2026-06-19 — Fix: TextHub inbox `received_at` parsed as UTC−6 (was UTC); backfill stage opt-out attribution — docs: 04/crons, 07-conventions, CHANGELOG
- TextHub stamps inbound STOP `received_at` in a fixed UTC−6 wall clock with no zone; we parsed it as UTC, putting the 72h-attribution anchor 6h in the past. A campaign's **own** STOP replies then failed the `sent_at <= anchor + 5min` upper bound and were dropped as "send-after-stop", so the stage opt-out counter read **0** while ~100 recipients had actually replied STOP (org-wide suppression was unaffected — only stage attribution under-counted). Confirmed empirically: our ingest clock ran a rock-solid ~6h ahead of the stamped value across 132 messages. Fix: `parseProviderReceivedAt` shifts +6h via `TEXTHUB_RECEIVED_AT_UTC_OFFSET_HOURS = -6` ([`lib/sends/poll-opt-outs.ts`](../lib/sends/poll-opt-outs.ts)); zone-bearing ISO inputs are honored as-is. Test updated ([`scripts/verify-optout-attribution.ts`](../scripts/verify-optout-attribution.ts), all green).
- Backfilled historical attribution via [`scripts/backfill-optout-attributions.ts`](../scripts/backfill-optout-attributions.ts) (anchors on `opt_outs.created_at`, the reliable UTC ingest clock, so it's correct independent of the parse bug): **482 new `opt_out_attributions` rows, 13 stage counters corrected**. Example: stage `8_62_061826_1_s2_c122` 0 → 112 inbound-STOP attributions (104 distinct contacts). `opt_out_count` (CSV/manual-results column) deliberately untouched to avoid clobbering imported results; the dedicated `inbound_opt_out_count` — read by the Reports page and the campaign "Inbound STOPs" metric — is what was fixed. DST caveat noted in docs (offset measured in June).

## 2026-06-19 — Perf batch 3: batch the campaigns/stages audience-count N+1 (behavior-preserving) — docs: 04/audience-snapshot, CHANGELOG
- `GET /api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages` computed `audience_count` with one query per stage (a full `campaign_audience_pool` scan + per-contact opt-out check each) — the app's slowest page. Replaced with two new batched functions in [`lib/audience-snapshot.ts`](../lib/audience-snapshot.ts): `computeStageAudienceCountsBatch` (active/frozen-pool) and `computeStageAudienceCountsBatchForDraft` (draft projection), which count ALL non-lane stages in a **single** pass (pool/source scanned once; per-stage filters + split bucket via `ROW_NUMBER() PARTITION BY stage_id` conditional aggregation). Behavioral-lane stages keep their unchanged per-lane `countStageRecipients`. The three original count functions are untouched (other routes use them). Route now sets `maxDuration = 30`.
- **Hard rule met — exact equivalence proven on real data before ship:** old vs new compared for every stage of every campaign — **211/211 stages numerically identical** (active 111 incl. opt-outs-in-pool / mid-send / lane cases; draft 100 incl. segment-only / group-only / both-∩ / 25 splits / 59 cap-clamps). Opt-out/suppression and attribution semantics unchanged — only counting performance. The draft batch CTE is `MATERIALIZED` after the proof caught a `statement_timeout` regression (planner re-evaluated the segment-rule source per stage in one statement); re-ran the full proof after the fix to confirm counts unaffected.

## 2026-06-19 — Perf batch 1: auth memoization + middleware /api exclusion + server-hydrated strips + additive indexes (migration 0078) — docs: 03-data-model, 04/multi-tenancy-auth, CHANGELOG
- **Auth tax (no behavior change).** `getUser()` / `getOrgMembership()` ([`lib/auth/helpers.ts`](../lib/auth/helpers.ts)) and the API-side user/membership primitives ([`lib/api/helpers.ts`](../lib/api/helpers.ts)) are now wrapped in `React.cache()` — the `supabase.auth.getUser()` network round-trip and the `org_members` lookup run at most once per server request. `proxy.ts` middleware matcher now excludes **all of `api/`** (was only `api/webhooks/`): every API route self-authenticates in its own handler (audited — 172/172 routes), so the middleware `getUser()` was a redundant second round-trip per API call. The protected layout server-hydrates `<AuthProvider>` and the send-state strip (`<SendStateStripLoader>` in `<Suspense>`) so neither re-fetches `/api/me` or `/api/sends/state` (each a full auth round-trip) on mount; `/api/sends/state`'s query set was extracted to [`lib/sends/send-state.ts`](../lib/sends/send-state.ts) (endpoint response unchanged). `getSession()` was **deliberately not** substituted for `getUser()` on any path — Supabase warns it doesn't revalidate the JWT server-side, and memoization already removes the redundancy.
- **Additive indexes (migration `0078`, no semantic change).** `opt_outs(org_id, contact_id)` (compliance-critical suppression table — index only), `stage_result_rows(org_id, created_at)`, `contacts(org_id, created_at)`, partial `campaign_stages(scheduled_at) WHERE sent_at IS NULL AND schedule_missed_at IS NULL`, `campaigns(org_id, status)`, and partial `stage_sends(org_id) WHERE status='sending'` (the `/api/sends/state` stuck-row count on every protected page — `stage_sends` already had `(org_id, sent_at)` but nothing for the `status='sending'` probe).

## 2026-06-19 — Fix activation timeout: materialize + ANALYZE the audience candidate set — docs: 04/audience-snapshot, CHANGELOG
- Activating a campaign over a large contact group (≈150K members) timed out with Postgres `statement timeout (57014)` and a 500. Root cause: the candidate set is built from `UNION`/`INTERSECT`/`EXCEPT` set ops, whose output cardinality the planner can't estimate (defaults to ~200 rows); the misestimate made it pick nested-loop anti-joins for the opt-out / in-use exclusions, scanning ~candidates × active-pool rows. Fix: `snapshotAudience` ([`lib/audience-snapshot.ts`](../lib/audience-snapshot.ts)) now materializes the candidate set into a `ON COMMIT DROP` temp table and `ANALYZE`s it before the flag joins, and stages the qualified rows in a second temp table so count + the random-sample insert share one evaluation. Measured >180s (timeout) → ~8.5s for a 150K-candidate / 5K-cap activation. Both snapshot routes (`/api/campaigns` POST, `/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/status`) now set `maxDuration = 60`. Preview/draft-stage-count paths share the root cause but run outside a transaction and are left as a noted follow-up.

## 2026-06-19 — Stage Results: positive-only Keitaro guard + history backfill — docs: 04/keitaro-poll, CHANGELOG
- Hardened the Keitaro counter sync so it overwrites `click_count`/`checkout_click_count`/`sales_count` **only when Keitaro reports a value > 0** for that field — a Keitaro 0 no longer zeroes an existing manual/CSV number (a stage with tracked clicks but no Keitaro-reported sales keeps its manual sales). Same guard in [`scripts/backfill-stage-results.ts`](../scripts/backfill-stage-results.ts), an idempotent one-time backfill that mirrors stored Keitaro totals + TextHub `inbound_opt_out_count` into the per-stage counters for existing campaigns. (The prior unconditional overwrite could zero manual sales because Keitaro currently reports 0 sales.)

## 2026-06-19 — Stage Results auto-fill from Keitaro + TextHub; remove Late Clickers (migration 0077) — docs: 03-data-model, 04/keitaro-poll, 04/csv-imports, 04/campaigns-stages-creatives, CHANGELOG
- The per-stage **Results** panel now auto-populates: the Keitaro `*/5` poll overwrites `campaign_stages.click_count` (= landing-page `visit_clicks_clean`, label renamed **"Clicker 1st Day" → "Clickers"**), `checkout_click_count` (= `checkouts`), and `sales_count` (= `sales`) for tracked stages (summed across dates; `sales_payout_each` snapshotted from offer CPA on first sale); the opt-out poller mirrors `inbound_opt_out_count` → `opt_out_count` so **Opt-outs** reflect live TextHub STOPs. SMS sent / Delivered / Scrubbed / Bounced / Total Cost stay manual/CSV. The manual form & CSV import still write the auto-owned counters, but the next poll wins for stages with upstream data (fallback for untracked stages).
- **Removed "Late Clickers" entirely** (migration `0077`, **destructive — apply after the deploy that stops reading the columns**): dropped `campaign_stages.late_click_count`, `stage_results_imports.late_clickers_added`, and `stage_results_imports.clicker_phase` (+ its CHECK). The clicker-only "late" CSV import phase and all its UI (toggle, preview card, history badge) are gone; imports are now a single full pass.

## 2026-06-19 — Creatives: new manual `funnel_stage` field + list filter (migration 0076) — docs: 03-data-model, 04/campaigns-stages-creatives, CHANGELOG
- Added a manual `funnel_stage` metadata field to `creatives` — `start`/`clicked`/`checkout`/`ignored`/`unknown` (default `unknown`), modeled exactly on `quality`: a `TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'unknown'` column with a CHECK constraint (migration `0076`, **must be applied before deploy**). Wired end-to-end: create/update/bulk-create/bulk-edit schemas + forms, the `/api/creatives/list` `funnel_stage` query-param filter (multi, comma-separated) + sort column, a `MultiSelectPicker` filter on the `/creatives` list, and a sortable "Funnel Stage" table column. Not enforced anywhere else in the system.

## 2026-06-18 — Inbound STOP → campaign/stage attribution (migration 0075) — docs: 03-data-model (+ERD), 04/crons, 04/keitaro-poll, 05-flows, CHANGELOG
- TextHub's inbox carries no campaign reference, so each inbound STOP is reverse-matched to sends by phone + recency: the opt-out poller now credits **every stage that sent to the number within a 72h trailing window** (`OPT_OUT_ATTRIBUTION_WINDOW_HOURS`), writing one `opt_out_attributions` row per stage and bumping `campaign_stages.inbound_opt_out_count`, all inside the existing per-message transaction. Org-wide `opt_outs` suppression unchanged — attribution is additive. The Reports "Opt-outs" column now reads `inbound_opt_out_count` (live STOPs), and the campaign page "Inbound STOPs" rollup shows DISTINCT attributed contacts (window semantics over-count a naive per-stage sum). New table + 2 columns on `texthub_inbound_events` + 1 counter on `campaign_stages` + lookup index (migration 0075, **must be applied before deploy**). Backfill: `scripts/backfill-optout-attributions.ts`. Replaces the prior single-most-recent live attribution on the campaign page.

## 2026-06-18 — Reports: per-stage Opt-outs column + clickable Campaign/Stage cells — docs: 04/keitaro-poll, CHANGELOG
- `/api/keitaro/reports` now returns `opt_outs` per row (the existing `campaign_stages.opt_out_count`, captured once per stage — never summed across the per-day rows) plus in grand totals, and `opt_outs` is sortable. The Reports page adds an **Opt-outs** column. Campaign cell → `/campaigns/[id]`; Stage cell → `/campaigns/[id]?stage=[stageId]`, which the campaign detail page reads on load to auto-open that stage's editor (no standalone stage route exists). No schema change.

## 2026-06-18 — Per-second send rate moved to the PHONE NUMBER level — migrations 0073 (+0074 drops the provider col) — docs: 03-data-model, 04/sms-send-pipeline, CHANGELOG
- The per-second rate is a carrier limit that depends on the number TYPE, and one provider can own numbers of different types (TextHub: short code 60/s, toll-free 3/s). So it belongs on `provider_phones`, not `sms_providers`. Migration 0073 adds `provider_phones.max_sends_per_second`; the drain resolves the rate from the stage's `provider_phone_id` (`campaign_stages.provider_phone_id` → `provider_phones.max_sends_per_second`), `LEFT JOIN provider_phones`. Editable per number in the phone dialog (`phone-form.tsx`); removed from the provider dialog. The provider-level `max_sends_per_run` ceiling raise (2000→20000) from the prior change is kept.
- Expand/contract: 0073 (add phone col) applied + the live TextHub phones set (short code #26 = 60, toll-free #27 = 3) BEFORE deploying the code that reads it; the now-orphan `sms_providers.max_sends_per_second` (added same day in 0072, never used in prod) is dropped by migration 0074 applied AFTER deploy, so no in-flight drain hits a missing column. verify-drain green (incl pacing) + scheduled + integrity + tsc.

## 2026-06-18 — Per-provider send rate limit (max_sends_per_second) + per-second pacing — migration 0072 — docs: 03-data-model, 04/sms-send-pipeline, CHANGELOG
- TextHub confirmed a hard per-SECOND rate (60/s short code, 3/s toll free). None of the existing caps bound the instantaneous rate, so the drain could burst past it and draw rate-limit rejections. Added `sms_providers.max_sends_per_second` (migration 0072, nullable) — set per provider in Settings (new "Max per second" field in the provider dialog).
- Drain ([lib/sends/drain.ts](../lib/sends/drain.ts)) now sizes its parallel slice to `rate = resolveSendsPerSecond(max_sends_per_second)` (default 10, clamped ≤1000) and **paces** each slice so it occupies ≥ slice/rate seconds — sustained throughput never exceeds the provider's per-second limit. Composes with the per-minute/24h volume caps ([lib/sends/circuit-breakers.ts](../lib/sends/circuit-breakers.ts)).
- Raised `ABSOLUTE_MAX_SENDS_PER_RUN` 2000→20000 (+ the Zod validator) — the 2000 clamp was an artifact of the old serial drain; a 300s invocation can now complete ~18k at 60/s, so 2000 had become an artificial per-tick throttle. The per-second rate + 300s function budget are the real anti-runaway guards.
- Applied migration 0072 to the DB + set the live TextHub provider (short code) to 60/s; integrity green (72/72). verify-drain green (adds per-second resolver + a pacing assertion: 6 sends at rate 3 take ≥1.6s) + scheduled suite + tsc clean. **`SEND_ENABLED` is ON in prod** — live path.

## 2026-06-18 — Send drain: bulk-persist each slice (parallel sends weren't enough) — no schema change — docs: 04/sms-send-pipeline, CHANGELOG
- Live measurement after the first throughput fix showed the drain still pinned at **~2.5 sends/sec**: parallelizing only the network call left **~20 serial DB writes per slice** (2 per recipient) as the new bottleneck, and the `countSentSince` rate-ceiling reads were fast (~48ms) so they were NOT the cause. Fixed by persisting each slice in BULK — `≤2 UPDATE … FROM (VALUES …)` (sent / failed-or-filtered) + one multi-row `send_attempts` INSERT — instead of per-recipient round-trips ([lib/sends/drain.ts](../lib/sends/drain.ts)). Counting + the failure-spike breaker still fold results in claimed order (JS), and every write is a single statement (safe on pool AND a single-connection tx).
- [scripts/verify-drain.ts](../scripts/verify-drain.ts) now runs under a **throwaway org created inside the rolled-back tx** instead of the first live org. The rate-ceiling breakers count `stage_sends` org-wide; with live campaigns now sending continuously (see the `*/5` change below), a shared org pushed `countSentSince()` over the test caps and made assertions flap. The dedicated org isolates the counts. verify-drain green 5× + scheduled suite green + `tsc` clean.
- Reminder still applies: `SEND_ENABLED` is **ON** in production — this is a live send path.

## 2026-06-17 — Send drain: concurrent recipient sends (throughput fix) + send-scheduled cron `*/15`→`*/5` — no schema change — docs: 04/sms-send-pipeline, 04/crons, CHANGELOG
- **Root cause of slow sends:** `runStageDrain` ([lib/sends/drain.ts](../lib/sends/drain.ts)) awaited each TextHub round-trip + 2 DB writes strictly serially (~2 sends/sec), so a run hit the 300s function timeout at ~600 sends regardless of the raised `max_sends_per_run`/minute caps — the caps were never the limiter, wall-clock throughput was.
- **Fix:** each claimed batch now fires its TextHub sends in **slices of `concurrency`** (`DEFAULT_SEND_CONCURRENCY = 10`, [lib/sends/circuit-breakers.ts](../lib/sends/circuit-breakers.ts); injectable via `opts.concurrency`). **Only the network call is parallelized** (`Promise.all` over the slice's `sendSms`); the DB writes (status update + `send_attempts` evidence) and the failure-spike breaker stay **strictly serial in claimed order**, so breaker semantics are unchanged AND no concurrent statements ever hit one connection — correct whether `dbc` is the pool (cron/drain) or a single-connection tx (tests). With ~400ms TextHub latency, 10 parallel sends ≈ ~20 sends/sec. Conservative default pending confirmation of TextHub's documented per-key rate limit; the per-minute/24h ceilings remain the policy-rate backstops.
- `vercel.json`: `send-scheduled` cron `*/15`→`*/5` so large scheduled audiences drain faster across (resumable) ticks.
- Tests: [scripts/verify-drain.ts](../scripts/verify-drain.ts) — added a concurrency assertion (max in-flight bounded by `concurrency`, >1 proves non-serial), run 5× for stability after an earlier all-parallel design proved flaky on the test harness's single-connection tx; full suite + scheduled-send tests green; `tsc` clean. NOTE: `SEND_ENABLED` is **ON** in production — this deploys to a live send path.

## 2026-06-17 — Behavioral-branching: lanes send through the existing pipeline (build step 6) — no schema change — docs: 04-features/behavioral-lanes, CHANGELOG
- `kickoffStageSend` ([lib/sends/kickoff.ts](../lib/sends/kickoff.ts)) and `preflightStageSend` ([lib/sends/preflight.ts](../lib/sends/preflight.ts)) now select `behavioral_tier` + `parent_stage_id` and pass them into the SAME `stageRecipientsSql` the stages-list live preview uses — so for a lane, the people SENT (materialized into `stage_sends`) are byte-identical to the people PREVIEWED. No parallel send path; a lane is a stage with a narrower recipient set. **Sending remains fully gated** — `SEND_ENABLED` env default unchanged, no live fire.
- Every safety layer still gates lane sends (all in `runStageDrain`, untouched): `send_approved`, `SEND_ENABLED`, per-org `sends_enabled`, provider `send_paused`, credentials, pacing/minute/24h breakers, opt-out suppression (inside the recipient query), and `stage_sends` at-most-once (kickoff `already_pending` guard + the partial unique index). Lane `stage_sends`/links rows are written identically, so downstream tier/aliveness reads feed the next position.
- Tests: [scripts/test-lane-send.ts](../scripts/test-lane-send.ts) — 24/24 under a throwaway org (SEND_ENABLED off, kickoff dry-materialize, drain exercised with gate OFF + a mock dispatcher that throws if invoked): preview==send per lane, opt-out/converted excluded at send resolution, gate-off dispatches nothing (mock never called), at-most-once on re-kickoff, lane rows shaped like an ordinary stage's, and a lane send feeds a next-position aliveness read; real-data counts unchanged.

Same confirm → endpoint → refetch flow and the same gating (hidden on lanes and already-split parents). Lane table rendering + explainer callout unchanged.

## 2026-06-17 — Behavioral-branching: second entry point in the stage editor — docs: 04-features/behavioral-lanes, CHANGELOG
- Added a **"Behavioral split…"** button inside the stage editor's audience block, beside "Split for A/B test…" ([components/campaigns/stage-form.tsx](../components/campaigns/stage-form.tsx)), in addition to the existing stage-row action. Shown only when editing an ordinary stage (hidden on lanes, with a "this is a behavioral lane" note instead). It hands off to the parent, which runs the same confirm dialog + endpoint as the row action — one shared implementation, two entry points. ## 2026-06-17 — Behavioral-branching: operator UI + live preview counts (build step 5) — no schema change — docs: 04-features/behavioral-lanes (new), CHANGELOG
- Campaign detail page ([app/(protected)/campaigns/[id]/page.tsx](../app/(protected)/campaigns/[id]/page.tsx)): a **"Behavioral split…"** action in the stage row menu (ordinary stages only — hidden on lanes and on stages that already have lanes), with a confirm dialog mirroring the A/B split flow; calls the step-4 endpoint and the three lanes appear. Lane rows render a tier chip (`↳ Ignored`/`Clicked`/`Reached offer` · from #N), parents show an `N behavioral lanes` badge, and an explainer notes converted exit + opted-out suppression (why lane counts don't sum to the pool).
- **Live preview counts** reuse the existing recipient path — no second query: the stages list route ([route](../app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/route.ts)) now computes a lane row's `audience_count` via the new `countStageRecipients()` ([lib/sends/recipients.ts](../lib/sends/recipients.ts)), which wraps the step-3 `stageRecipientsSql` lane logic (alive + exact tier − opt-outs, converted excluded). Lane rows always show the number (even `0`) tagged `live`. With no live sends ever fired, lanes preview 0 today (nobody is "alive" yet) — renders cleanly.
- Immutability confirmed/hardened: `behavioral_tier`/`parent_stage_id` were already un-PATCHable (not in `stageUpdateSchema`; Zod strips them) — added to the PATCH route's `NON_UPDATABLE` as an explicit backstop. List + detail now carry the two fields. New feature doc: [04-features/behavioral-lanes.md](04-features/behavioral-lanes.md).
