## 2026-06-17 — Behavioral-branching: behavioral-split endpoint (build step 4) — no schema change — docs: CHANGELOG only
- New endpoint `POST /api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/[stageId]/behavioral-split` ([route](../app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/[stageId]/behavioral-split/route.ts)). Turns the chosen stage into a PARENT position (it stays ordinary) and stamps three lane-stages under it — `behavioral_tier` 0/1/2 (ignored/clicked/reached); tier 3 converted gets no lane. Each lane clones the parent's config, sets `behavioral_tier` + `parent_stage_id = chosen stage`, regenerates its own stage tracking_id, and leaves `split_index/split_total` NULL (lanes partition by tier, not the equal-size A/B partition). Transactional — a partial split can't persist. Modeled on the A/B split route's transaction/clone structure.
- Logic factored into [lib/stages/behavioral-split.ts](../lib/stages/behavioral-split.ts) (`performBehavioralSplit`) so it's testable without the auth session; the route is a thin auth (`stages.create`) + error-map wrapper. Guards: reject if the target is itself a lane (`already_lane`), archived, or already behaviorally split (`already_behaviorally_split`). **No draft/status gate** — the A/B split route has none either, and behavioral lanes are created post-activation by design. utm_tag_ids is cloned too (A/B split omits it; included here for config completeness).
- NOT wired into UI/sending yet. Tests: [scripts/test-behavioral-split.ts](../scripts/test-behavioral-split.ts) — 16/16 under a dedicated throwaway org (teardown scoped to org_id, marker-guarded; real-data counts asserted unchanged): 3 lanes with tiers 0/1/2 + correct parent + distinct tracking IDs + NULL split fields; splitting a lane rejected; the step-1 CHECK rejects tier-set/parent-NULL and tier=3; and a forced mid-transaction tracking_id collision rolls back with zero orphan lanes.

## 2026-06-17 — Behavioral-branching: tier + aliveness in the recipient query (build step 3) — no schema change — docs: CHANGELOG only
- Extended the single recipient choke-point `stageRecipientsSql()` in [lib/sends/recipients.ts](../lib/sends/recipients.ts) with two NULL-guarded overlays that activate only when the stage is a behavioral lane (`behavioralTier` set on the filters): **(1) aliveness** — `EXISTS` a `stage_sends` row for `parentStageId` with `status='sent'` (tracked-only; shaped so a `stage_result_rows outcome='delivered'` source unions in later with no caller change); **(2) exact tier match** — LEFT JOIN `campaignTierExpr` (step 2), `coalesce(tier,0) = behavioralTier`, plus a global `coalesce(tier,0) <> 3` guard so converted contacts never appear in any lane. Two new OPTIONAL filter fields `behavioralTier?`/`parentStageId?`; ordinary callers (kickoff, export, audience-count) pass neither, so the emitted SQL is **byte-identical** to the pre-lane version (verified: rendered SQL + params equal). The frozen `campaign_audience_pool` stays the universe — tier + aliveness are LIVE overlays read at query time, like the existing live opt_outs check; the pool is never re-snapshotted.
- NOT wired into any send/preview/UI path yet (no caller sets the lane fields). Tests: [scripts/test-recipients-lanes.ts](../scripts/test-recipients-lanes.ts) — 12/12 against synthetic data seeded under a dedicated throwaway org (torn down by org_id only; real-data table counts asserted unchanged): ordinary unchanged, tier-0/1/2 lanes correct, cross-lane climb (clicked→reached lands in tier-2 not tier-1), converted in no lane, opted-out in no lane, not-alive (no parent send) in no lane, and lanes ∪ {converted} == alive-and-not-opted-out (mutually exclusive, no double-count/loss). Full feature doc (04-features) deferred to the wiring step.

## 2026-06-17 — Behavioral-branching: campaign tier fragment (build step 2) — no schema/behavior change — docs: CHANGELOG only
- New reusable SQL builder `campaignTierExpr(campaignId, orgId)` in [lib/campaign-tier.ts](../lib/campaign-tier.ts): returns a subquery `(contact_id, tier)` for each contact's CURRENT high-water tier within ONE campaign — 0 ignored / 1 clean click on a campaign link / 2 `stage_sends.offer_reached_at` / 3 `stage_sends.sale_status='sale'`. High-water = MAX over a UNION of per-signal sources; campaign-scoped; read live (Option A); absence ⇒ COALESCE to 0 at the caller. "Clean click" mirrors the click report (excludes bot/prefetch/suspect). Manual-mode signals union in later without reshaping callers. NOT yet wired into `stageRecipientsSql` or any send/preview path — built and tested in isolation. Tests: [scripts/test-campaign-tier.ts](../scripts/test-campaign-tier.ts) (11/11 against seeded synthetic data, fixtures torn down). No migration, no live-path change.

## 2026-06-17 — Behavioral-branching lanes: schema groundwork (build step 1) — migration 0071 — docs: 03-data-model
- Migration `0071_stage_behavioral_lanes.sql`: two NULLABLE `campaign_stages` columns — `behavioral_tier` (lane target: 0 ignored / 1 clicked / 2 reached; tier 3 "converted" exits, never a lane) and `parent_stage_id` (self-FK, ON DELETE CASCADE; the PRIOR-position stage, used ONLY for the aliveness check, NOT a "was-in-this-lane-before" link). Coherence `campaign_stages_behavioral_lane_check`: both NULL (ordinary stage = today's behavior) or `behavioral_tier IN (0,1,2)` + `parent_stage_id` set. Sparse index `campaign_stages_parent_stage_id_idx WHERE parent_stage_id IS NOT NULL`. Non-destructive: every existing stage gets both columns NULL, so non-behavioral stages are unaffected.
- Ordering decision: **added `parent_stage_id`** rather than reuse `stage_number`. `stage_number` is trigger-assigned creation order and behavioral lanes share one logical position while taking distinct stage_numbers, so it can't name "the prior position" without a sibling-grouping concept we deliberately dropped (chaining forbidden ⇒ no `split_group_id`). Same-campaign parentage, "parent is not itself a lane", and the no-chaining rule are enforced in the API (a CHECK can't reference another row). Schema + snapshot reconciled with the migration; recipient resolution / split button NOT built yet (later steps).

## 2026-06-17 — Segment "reached the offer page" rule types (engagement Level 2) — migration 0070 — docs: 03-data-model, 04/audience-segments, 04/keitaro-poll, 04/crons, 06-integrations
- Added three segment rule types — `reached_offer` / `reached_offer_for_brand` / `reached_offer_for_offer` — matching contacts with ≥1 `stage_sends` row where `offer_reached_at IS NOT NULL`. Both `is` (reached) / `is_not` (didn't reach); brand/offer scoping joins `stage_sends → campaigns`, mirroring the clicker/purchase rules. Source of truth `RULE_TYPES` in [lib/validators/segment-rule-types.ts](../lib/validators/segment-rule-types.ts); eval cases in [lib/segment-rules-eval.ts](../lib/segment-rules-eval.ts). **Headline:** "reached the offer page but did NOT buy" = `reached_offer` is + `made_purchase` is_not. Empty until real sends accumulate (an empty preview = "no one reached the offer yet", not a bug).
- New per-recipient offer-reach poll: [lib/keitaro/poll-offer-reaches.ts](../lib/keitaro/poll-offer-reaches.ts) + route `app/api/keitaro/poll-offer-reaches` + `*/15` Vercel cron. Reads Keitaro `clicks/log` (new client `fetchKeitaroClicks`), drops `gk-lp-visits` landing clicks, stamps `stage_sends.offer_reached_at` + `offer_reach_event_id` for offer-campaign clicks whose `sub_id_1` maps to a recipient. Reuses the conversions poll's rolling-window / dedup / never-throw / ET→UTC-cast pattern verbatim. Confirmed live: an offer-campaign click carries the same `sub_id_1` as the recipient's landing click.
- Migration `0070_stage_send_offer_reach.sql`: two nullable `stage_sends` columns + partial index + restated `segment_rules_rule_type_check` (adds the 3 types). Non-destructive. Tests: [scripts/test-offer-reach-rules.ts](../scripts/test-offer-reach-rules.ts) (12/12, incl. empty-data + the headline "reached but didn't buy = the non-buyer only" + brand/offer scoping).

## 2026-06-17 — Segment "made a purchase" rule types (engagement Level 3) — migration 0069 — docs: 03-data-model, 04/audience-segments, 07-conventions
- Added three segment rule types — `made_purchase` / `made_purchase_for_brand` / `made_purchase_for_offer` — matching contacts with ≥1 `stage_sends` row where `sale_status = 'sale'` (`'lead'`/`'rejected'` excluded). Both `is` (bought) and `is_not` (didn't buy); brand/offer scoping joins `stage_sends → campaigns`, mirroring the clicker rules. Source of truth `RULE_TYPES` in [lib/validators/segment-rule-types.ts](../lib/validators/segment-rule-types.ts); eval cases in [lib/segment-rules-eval.ts](../lib/segment-rules-eval.ts). Empty until real sales accumulate (an empty preview = "no buyers yet", not a bug). Level 1 (clicker rules) already supported include/exclude — no change. Level 2 (offer-page reach) intentionally NOT built (only exists in Keitaro per-stage aggregates, not per-contact). Tests: [scripts/test-purchase-rules.ts](../scripts/test-purchase-rules.ts) (12/12).
- Migration `0069_segment_rules_purchase_types.sql` restates the `segment_rules_rule_type_check` IN-list (drop/re-add CHECK, non-destructive) and reconciles `db/schema.ts` + the snapshot with the live constraint — closing the long-standing `is_in_contact_group` drift (conventions gotcha #2 now RESOLVED).

## 2026-06-17 — Tracked clicks now feed clicker segment rules — no migration — docs: 04/audience-segments, 04/crons
- **Bug fix.** Clicker segment rules (`is_clicker_any_brand` / `_for_brand` / `_for_offer`) read the `clickers` table, which was only ever populated by CSV upload — so contacts who clicked real tracked SMS links (rows in `clicks`) never matched, and clicker segments returned 0 despite visible click activity. New [lib/links/propagate-clickers.ts](../lib/links/propagate-clickers.ts) bridges the gap: it materializes one `clickers` row (`source='tracked_click'`) per `(contact, brand, offer)` for every **clean** click (`classification='human' AND scored_at IS NOT NULL` — same definition as the default clicker export; suspect/prefetch/bot excluded), deriving brand/offer/provider attribution from the link's campaign + stage. Runs after scoring in the `score-pending` cron (best-effort, idempotent). Backfill via [scripts/backfill-tracked-clickers.ts](../scripts/backfill-tracked-clickers.ts) (one-time, idempotent). Verified against live data: 76 Guide-Kin clickers materialized from 84 human clicks, idempotent re-run inserts 0, per-brand/offer counts match the click log.

## 2026-06-17 — Per-recipient sale attribution via Keitaro sub_id_1 — migration 0067 — docs: 03, 04/keitaro-poll, 04/tracking-attribution, 05, 06, 07
- Maps individual Keitaro **sales** back to the **phone** that received the SMS. The per-recipient id is `stage_sends.id` (= link `send_token`), appended to the tracked link as `&sub_id1=<send_token>` at **redirect time** ([lib/links/resolve-click.ts](../lib/links/resolve-click.ts), `RECIPIENT_SUB_ID_PARAM`) — the shared per-stage destination and the operator's Full URL are untouched. A new `*/15` conversions poll ([lib/keitaro/poll-conversions.ts](../lib/keitaro/poll-conversions.ts), `POST /admin_api/v1/conversions/log`, endpoint `/api/keitaro/poll-conversions`) folds the latest conversion per `sub_id_1` and stamps `stage_sends.sale_status`/`sale_revenue`/`converted_at`/`keitaro_conversion_id` (migration 0067, four nullable cols + CHECK + partial index). **Dedup on Keitaro `event_id`**; one sale per recipient, latest wins (not cumulative — ledger upgrade path documented). **Sale** badge added to the Activity → Messages list. Live-confirmed conversions/log schema: `revenue` (not `payout`), `event_id` (UUIDv7, dedup key), `datetime` ET wall-clock; the endpoint returns only requested columns and 400s on unknown ones / an `order` key. Spelling split documented: URL param `sub_id1` (no underscore) ↔ Keitaro token `sub_id_1`. Verified: redirect 302 carries both `sub_id3`+`sub_id1` (Playwright + direct), live poll (0 writes pre-deploy), match/stamp/dedup + ET→UTC tz correctness via rolled-back txns, migration-integrity green.

## 2026-06-17 — Enable RLS on geoip_cache (Supabase advisor rls_disabled_in_public) — migration 0066 — docs: 03, 07
- The only `public` table with RLS disabled. It's server-only infra cache (MaxMind `.mmdb` blob, no `org_id`) accessed exclusively via the Drizzle/postgres-js direct connection in [lib/links/geoip-cache.ts](../lib/links/geoip-cache.ts) — no anon/SSR/browser client touches it. Migration `0066_geoip_cache_rls.sql` runs `ALTER TABLE public.geoip_cache ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` with **no policies** (Case A): the DB role and `service_role` bypass RLS so server code is unchanged, while anon/authenticated PostgREST access is default-denied. Verified: 0 public tables RLS-disabled, `verify-geoip-cache` (read+write path) green, migration-integrity green.

## 2026-06-16 — SMS pipeline WS2b/c/d: collapsed "Approve Send" + abort

Shared `runStageDrainAndRecord` ([lib/sends/drain-and-record.ts](../lib/sends/drain-and-record.ts)) + `KICKOFF_REFUSAL` extracted so the drain route, approve-send, and kickoff route can't drift. ## 2026-06-16 — SMS pipeline WS2a: scheduler drain decoupling (foundation)
- `selectDrainableStages` ([lib/sends/scheduled.ts](../lib/sends/scheduled.ts)) decoupled from materialization: Phase B drains only **released** (`sent_at` set) or **due** (`scheduled_at <= now`) stages, with a per-stage in-window gate (first-fire = day-anchored `decideScheduledSend` + release stamp; continuation = `isOutsideSendWindow` hold). A future-armed pre-materialized stage is now held until its time (the WS2 landmine). New `drain_held` counter. Backward-compatible (old flow stamps `sent_at` in Phase A → drains via the released branch). Verified: `test-scheduled-decouple` + existing budget/claim/reschedule/run all green. Docs: [04-features/sms-send-pipeline.md](04-features/sms-send-pipeline.md). (WS2b/c/d — collapsed Approve-Send UX + abort — to follow.)

## 2026-06-15 — Keitaro Step 5b fix: classify visit campaign by NAME not alias
- `buildVisitClassifier` ([lib/keitaro/poll.ts](../lib/keitaro/poll.ts)) was matching the visit campaign on `alias === "gk-lp-visits"`, but in the live panel `gk-lp-visits` is the campaign's **name** — its alias is a random code (`ZttBSV`). Zero matches ⇒ every click fell through to "redirect" and Clickers stuck at 0. Now matches on **name** (trimmed, case-insensitive), resolves it → its `campaign_id`(s) once, and classifies rows by `campaign_id`. Constant renamed `KEITARO_VISIT_CAMPAIGN_ALIAS` → `KEITARO_VISIT_CAMPAIGN_NAME`. No hardcoded id.
- Poll response gains `visit_campaigns_matched` (expect 1; 0 ⇒ visits can't be separated). Docs: [04-features/keitaro-poll.md](04-features/keitaro-poll.md), [04-features/crons.md](04-features/crons.md), [03-data-model.md](03-data-model.md), [06-integrations.md](06-integrations.md), [07-conventions.md](07-conventions.md).

## 2026-06-15 — Keitaro Step 5b: visit/redirect split + Reports UI
- Poll now groups Keitaro's report by `day` + `sub_id_3` + **`campaign_id`** and classifies each row by the Keitaro campaign **alias**: `gk-lp-visits` ⇒ landing-page **visits** ("Clickers"); any other campaign ⇒ **offer redirects** (+ sales/revenue/cost). The per-campaign rows are folded into one per-(stage, date) aggregate before UPSERT (multiple campaign rows now share one key). Classifier resolves alias via `GET /admin_api/v1/campaigns` (id→alias + name→alias); on failure rows fall back to redirect and the poll returns `classification_degraded:true`. Code: `lib/keitaro/{client,poll}.ts`, shared funnel math in new `lib/keitaro/funnel.ts`.
- Migration **0062_keitaro_visit_redirect_split** adds `visit_clicks_raw/clean` + `redirect_clicks_raw/clean`. Legacy `raw_clicks`/`clean_clicks` kept (mirrored = redirect) for back-compat; pre-5b rows fall back to treating those as redirect counts. **Not auto-applied** — run `npm run db:migrate` + `verify-migration-integrity.ts` against prod deliberately.
- New cross-campaign endpoint `GET /api/keitaro/reports` (date range ≤92d, search, sort, pagination, resolved campaign + stage names, grand totals) and a dedicated **Reports** page (`/reports`, nav under Campaigns) showing the Clickers → Offer Redirect → Sales funnel per stage with a manual "Refresh from Keitaro" (operator+). `GET /api/keitaro/results` reshaped to the funnel.
- Docs updated: [04-features/keitaro-poll.md](04-features/keitaro-poll.md) (§2b classification, §3 fold, §4 columns, §5/5b endpoints + UI), [04-features/crons.md](04-features/crons.md), [03-data-model.md](03-data-model.md), [06-integrations.md](06-integrations.md), [07-conventions.md](07-conventions.md).

## 2026-06-12 — Fix: scheduled tracked sends timed out + got stranded (full pipeline fix)
- **Root cause of "scheduled send never fired":** the cron materialized + sent a whole audience inside one 300s invocation, minting links **one-by-one** (~178s for 1000 recipients) AFTER committing the `sent_at` claim — so a timeout left the stage `sent_at`-locked with zero rows, and the scheduler never retried it. Three-part fix:
  1. **Batched minting** ([lib/links/mint-link.ts](../lib/links/mint-link.ts) `mintLinksBatch` + [lib/sends/kickoff.ts](../lib/sends/kickoff.ts) bulk inserts): single shared destination upsert + multi-row chunked `links`/`stage_sends` inserts. 1000-recipient kickoff ~178s → ~2-3s (measured).
  2. **Claim-after-materialize** ([lib/sends/scheduled.ts](../lib/sends/scheduled.ts)): `sent_at` is now stamped only AFTER kickoff succeeds; concurrency relies on the `(stage_id, contact_id)` active-send dedup index. A killed tick no longer strands the stage.
  3. **Resumable drain across ticks:** phase B (`selectDrainableStages`) drains any tracked stage with `pending` rows in budget-bounded batches, independent of `sent_at` — large audiences send across multiple `*/15` ticks instead of one 300s window.
  - Tests: rewrote `test-scheduled-budget`/`test-scheduled-run` to the two-phase counters; added `test-scheduled-e2e` (two-tick materialize→drain→resume, rolled back) and `verify-batch-kickoff` (timing/correctness); made `verify-send` fixture self-contained. — Docs updated: [04-features/sms-send-pipeline.md](04-features/sms-send-pipeline.md), [07-conventions.md](07-conventions.md).

## 2026-06-11 — Fix: manual "mark sent" silently cancelled scheduled tracked sends
- `campaign_stages.sent_at` is the `send-scheduled` cron's fire-lock (`selectDueScheduledStages` requires `sent_at IS NULL`). The legacy status action ([…/stages/[stageId]/status/route.ts](../app/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/stages/[stageId]/status/route.ts)) stamped the same column when moving a stage to `'sent'`, so marking a tracked stage `'sent'` before its scheduled time stamped `sent_at` and permanently removed it from the scheduler — and reverting the status didn't clear it (real incident: a 3 PM ET send never fired). Now the status route **rejects `'sent'` for tracked campaigns** (409 `tracked_stage_sent_is_pipeline_owned`), and the stage status dropdown hides `'sent'` for tracked campaigns ([campaigns/[id]/page.tsx](../app/(protected)/campaigns/[id]/page.tsx)). Added read-only `scripts/diagnose-scheduled.ts`. — Docs updated: [04-features/sms-send-pipeline.md](04-features/sms-send-pipeline.md), [07-conventions.md](07-conventions.md).

Added an "ALL" checkbox (off by default) in the Offers panel that drives a new `include_all_offers` param on `/api/creatives/list` ([lib/creatives/list-filters.ts](../lib/creatives/list-filters.ts)): when `false`, `applies_to_all_offers` creatives are hidden until opted in; when true/absent, they're included (single `offer_id` path unchanged). — Docs updated: [04-features/campaigns-stages-creatives.md](04-features/campaigns-stages-creatives.md).

Added a multi-offer `offer_ids` (CSV) param to `/api/creatives/list` via [lib/creatives/list-filters.ts](../lib/creatives/list-filters.ts) (eligible = applies_to_all_offers OR junction to ANY selected offer; single `offer_id` unchanged). ## 2026-06-10 — Creatives list: 30-day performance columns (CTR / Checkout Rate / Sales CR / EPC)
- Added four sortable, server-ranked 30-day metric columns to the creatives list and removed the `Campaigns` (placeholder) and `Quality` table columns. The list endpoint ([app/api/creatives/list/route.ts](../app/api/creatives/list/route.ts)) joins two per-creative aggregates (stage counters + tracked clean clicks) and returns a `metrics` object; ratios are NULL ("—") when their denominator is 0. Clean clicks combine manual-mode stage clicks and tracked-mode clean clicks (bot/prefetch/suspect excluded); stage counters window on `campaign_stages.created_at`, tracked clicks on `clicks.clicked_at`. — Docs updated: [04-features/campaigns-stages-creatives.md](04-features/campaigns-stages-creatives.md).

## 2026-06-10 — Campaign audience preview: ~24× faster
- Audience preview/snapshot/draft-stage-count sped up from ~9s to ~0.4s on a 750K-contact org. Two changes in [lib/audience-snapshot.ts](../lib/audience-snapshot.ts) + [lib/segment-rules-eval.ts](../lib/segment-rules-eval.ts): (1) `buildSegmentAudienceClause` accepts an optional `restrictUniverse`, and the three audience functions pass the contact-group set when both dimensions are selected so a near-universal `is_not` rule no longer scans all contacts before the intersection narrows it; (2) opt-out/opt-in/clicker/in-use flags are LEFT-JOINed via deduped CTEs (`flagSetCtes`/`flagJoins`) instead of correlated `EXISTS` per row. Verified result-identical against a brute-force ground truth. Note: with both dimensions selected, the preview's `from_segments` now reflects the segment evaluated within the group (= the intersection). — Docs updated: [04-features/audience-snapshot.md](04-features/audience-snapshot.md).

## 2026-06-10 — Campaign audience: segment ∩ group (was union)
- Campaign audience composition now **intersects** the segment dimension with the contact-group dimension when both are selected (a contact must be in a selected segment AND a selected group); a single populated dimension is used alone. Previously the two were UNION'd. Affects `buildQualifyingContactsSql` (snapshot), `previewAudience` (editor preview), and `computeStageAudienceCountForDraft` (draft stage count) in [lib/audience-snapshot.ts](../lib/audience-snapshot.ts) via the new `buildAudienceSourceClause` helper. Preview `from_segments`/`from_groups` now report each side's pre-intersection pool; `total_matching`/`overlap` are the intersected audience. — Docs updated: [04-features/audience-snapshot.md](04-features/audience-snapshot.md), CLAUDE.md §10b.

## 2026-06-10 — Segment rule: in use in another campaign in last period
- New `in_use_in_campaign_last_period` segment rule type (migration `0059`): include (`is`) / exclude (`is_not`) contacts that were in use in another campaign within a fixed lookback window (`1d`/`3d`/`1w`/`2w`/`1m`/`3m`/`6m`/`1y`). "In use" = in a `campaign_audience_pool` for a campaign with `status` active/paused/completed and ≥1 live stage (draft/pending/sent/success), windowed on `campaigns.created_at`. New `campaign_use_period` value shape. — Docs updated: [04-features/audience-segments.md](04-features/audience-segments.md), [03-data-model.md](03-data-model.md).

## 2026-06-08 — Upload contacts onto a draft campaign
- New `POST /api/campaigns/[campaignId]/upload-contacts` + draft-only "Upload contacts" button on the campaign detail page: CSV/paste phone upload that upserts contacts, tags them into selected existing contact group(s), and UNIONs those groups into the campaign's `audience_contact_group_ids`. Draft-only (audience freezes at activation); requires `contacts.upload` + `campaigns.update`. — Docs updated: [04-features/campaigns-stages-creatives.md](04-features/campaigns-stages-creatives.md).

## 2026-08-13 — Final whole-branch review fixes for feat/segment-rule-sent-from-phone — docs: 04-features/audience-segments, CHANGELOG
- Documented `phone_type` and `carrier` in the rule-type table — this branch's migration `0129` (four-place-registration fix) makes both creatable for the first time; previously they were correctly omitted because `verifyValueOwnership` rejected them at creation.
- Replaced the guessed "may approach the 10s timeout" caveat with production measurements: selective number 80.9 ms, worst-case dominant number 1,806 ms (vs. 8,480 ms pre-index) — clears both the 10s preview timeout and the 60s `snapshotAudience` activation budget with wide margin.
- No schema/behavior change from the docs edits themselves; see the same-commit code fixes (schema.ts check-constraint + index drift, validator id ceiling, eval doc comment, rules-panel incomplete-check alignment, provider-phones org filter, backfill script header/citation/divergence-warning) in the commit this entry ships with.

> When you change behavior that a doc describes, update the doc **and** add an entry here in the same PR (Part B rule).
